## LETTER TO STAKEHOLDERS

Prometeon Tyre Group explores the Sustainable Management Model with governance tools to support maintenance and creation of values, relationships with Stakeholders and related connection with the development of financial, productive, intellectual, human, natural, social, and relational capitals.

Prometeon  Tyre  Group  reaffirms  its  support  of  the Ten  Principles  of  the  United  Nations  Global Compact in the areas of Human Rights, Labour, Environment and Anti-Corruption since 2019. Starting from  September  2020  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  became  formally  a  Founder  Member  of  the  United Nations Global Compact Network Italy. With this new status, Prometeon Tyre Group is committed to contribute to the realization of the Foundation's  goals participating to the institutional life implementing the planned initiatives. By incorporating the Ten Principles of the UNGC into strategies, policies,  and  procedures,  and  establishing  a  culture  of  integrity  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  is  not  only upholding  its  basic  responsibilities  to  people  and  planet,  but  also  setting  the  stage  for  long-term success.  In  this  annual  Communication  on  Progress,  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  describes  actions  to continually improve the integration of the Global Compact and its principles into its business strategy, culture,  and  daily  operations.  Prometeon Tyre Group  is  committed to the  UN  Global  Compact  and together with other stakeholders, they are improving working conditions and human rights along the supply chain in the long term. Moreover, since 2019, the Company is committed to concretely support the Sustainable Development Goals.

Starting from 2022 Prometeon Tyre Group decided to enhance its Sustainability Strategy and Model with the support of specific ESG Rating Assessment and the new application of the Global Reporting Initiative in the Corporate Social Responsibility giving the possibility to Prometeon Tyre Group to better identify the impacts, negative and positive, working on the mitigation of these latest through short- and long-term  goals  and  targets.  Furthermore,  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  with  the  support  of  the Risk Management Committee ascertained which were the Sustainability risks that needed to be monitored and mitigated.

Prometeon Tyre Group through short, medium, and long-term vision and strategy on impacts regarding economy, environment, and people, including impacts on their human rights, will be able to manage across  the  organization's  activities  and  business  relations hips  all  the  identified  impacts.  By  the application of its business strategy and business model aim to prevent negative impacts and achieve positive impacts on the economy, environment, and people the Company will mitigate the negative impacts. With the aim to reduce the impacts, the Company already defined short and medium-term strategic priorities for contributing to sustainable development.

In  2022, 12 new materials topics have been identified by Prometeon Tyre Group which have been divided in 3 main areas as shown here below:

Prometeon Tyre Group's growth is built on sustainability, and it is a vital part of the way this latest conduct  its  business.  Identifying  material  topics,  the  Company  will  be  committed  to  enhance  its Corporate Social Responsibility to mitigate all the impacts creating economic value for territory and stakeholders. What Sustainability means at Prometeon Tyre Group: ' The development that meets the present need without compromising the capacities of future generations '.

Roberto Righi, General Manager of Prometeon Tyre Group stated that : 'Over the next few years we will  be  focusing  on  balancing  technological  development and sustainability, understood as meaning respect both for the environment and for all the communities and people with whom the company works on a day-to-day basis. With the initiatives set in motion, Prometeon Tyre Group is ready to continue its sustainable growth journey.'

Prometeon Tyre Group's main challenges, goals, and targets regarding its contribution to sustainable development for the next year and the coming three to five years are related to Sustainable Linked loans obtained in 2021 which required to the Company to define target until 2025 for Environment (waste  reduction),  Social  (training  to  all  employees)  and  Governance  (audit  on  all  suppliers  of  raw materials  including  cobalt  and  minerals).  Furthermore,  with  the  Sustainability  Committee  held  by Headquarter in Milan and at local level in Turkey, Egypt and Brazil, the Company is settings long-terms targets until 2030 to reduce waste, water, energy and CO2 emissions.

With specific reference to environment, the Company understands clearly threats posed by climate change, an undeniable fact of the contemporary world with a high potential to lead to abrupt changes in ecosystems. Determined to take a proactive role in struggle to stop climate change, Prometeon Tyre Group is committed to strive for actions with concrete results within its business activities all along its value chain, aimed at achieving product life circularity. With this aim in mind the Company conducts continuous research &amp; development activities on innovative materials and cooperates with external parties to carry out ESG assessment to improve constantly its Corporate Social Responsibility.  As a Company of which corporate ethics foundations are laid down by the very being and protection of human rights, Prometeon Tyre Group endeavors to offer its best possible contribution to the people and supports local initiatives providing for humanitarian aid to persons negatively affected by conflicts.

## COMMITMENT OF PROMETEON TO SUSTAINABILITY

## INTRO

Prometeon Tyre Group explores the Sustainable Management Model with the governance tools to support maintenance and creation of values, relationships with Stakeholders and related connection with  the  development  of  financial,  productive,  intellectual,  human,  natural,  social  and  relational capitals.

## UNITED NATIONS GLOBAL COMPACT

Since Prometeon Tyre Group joined the United Nations Global Compact in August 2019, which offers leadership  guidelines  envisaged  to  inspire  advanced  and  innovative  Sustainability  Performance Management for business community, Prometeon continued to enhance its management capacity for the creation and maintenance of sustainable values. Every year, the United Nations Global Compact proposes a series of initiatives to provide support in the definition of strategies and partnerships for the pursuit of Sustainable Development Goals launched in September 2015 in New York with the aim of accompanying the activities of sustainable companies until 2030.

Moreover, from September 2020, with the status of Founder Member of the United Nations Global Compact Network Italy Prometeon Tyre Group is committed to contribute to the realization of the Foundation's goals and thus not only by supporting the organization economically but also actively. In 2022 the Company participated to institutional life implementing the planned initiatives.

The present Report reflects the integrated Sustainability model adopted by Prometeon Tyre Group in 2022 which is following the Ten Principles of the United Nations Global Compact (here in after 'UNGC').

<!-- image -->

## The Ten Principles of the UNGC are the following:

## Human Rights

- Principle 1: states that Businesses should support and respect the protection of internationally proclaimed human rights;
- Principle 2: requires Businesses to ensure that they are not complicit in human rights abuses.

## Labour

- Principle 3: Businesses should uphold the freedom of association and the effective recognition of the right to collective bargaining;
- Principle 4: the elimination of all forms of forced and compulsory labour;
- Principle 5: the effective abolition of child labour;
- Principle 6: the elimination of discrimination in respect of employment and occupation.

## Environment

- Principle 7: Businesses should support a precautionary approach to environmental challenges;
- Principle  8:  requires  Businesses  to  undertake  initiatives  to  promote  greater  environmental responsibility;
- Principle 9: encourage the development and diffusion of environmentally friendly technologies.

## Anti-Corruption

- Principle 10: Businesses should take part in fight against corruption in all its forms, including extortion and bribery.

The  over  mentioned  principles  are  derived  from:  the  Universal  Declaration  of  Human  Rights,  the International Labour Organization's Declaration on Fundamental Principles and Rights at Work, the Rio Declaration on Environment and Development, and the United Nations Convention against Corruption.

The foundations of corporate sustainability are laid by a company's value system and a principles -based approach to doing business which imply for that  company to  adopt  operating  standards that,  at  a minimum, meet fundamental responsibilities in the areas of human rights, labour, environment and anti-corruption. Responsible businesses enact the same values and principles wherever they have a presence and know that good practices in one area do not offset harm in another. By incorporating the Ten  Principles  of  the  UNGC  into  strategies,  policies  and  procedures,  and  establishing  a  culture  of integrity Prometeon Tyre Group is not only upholding its basic responsibilities to people and planet, but also setting the stage for long-term success .

"If our developments are not sustainable, then it means we will come to suffer at some point It may not be us, but the future generations will undoubtedly do.

Nicolas Marchi = Chief Internal Audit &amp; Sustainability Officer at Prometeon Tyre Group.

## UNITED NATIONS SUSTAINABLE DEVELOPMENT GOALS

<!-- image -->

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

Prometeon Tyre Group continues to be inspired by the seventeen Sustainable Development Goals of the  United  Nations  (here  in  after  'SDGs')  designed  to  accompany  the  activities  of  sustainable companies up to 2030. The SDGs are the blueprints to achieve a better and more sustainable future for all that address the global challenges and that companies have to face out, including those related to poverty, inequality, climate change, environmental degradation, peace and justice. The 17 Goals are all interconnected, and in order to leave no one behind, it is important that all the companies work to achieve them all by 2030.

## PROMETEON TYRE GROUP SUSTAINABILITY PLAN AND THE SDGs

The Company in 2021 started to review its targets and Sustainability Plan implementing new activities such  as ESG  Rating  assessment,  Sustainability  linked  loans and  others  that  will  be  discussed  and presented in the Sustainability report of 2022. The aim is to follow the request regarding the global effort required to achieve the SDGs. In particular, it is possible to note the strong connection between the Sustainability Plan adopted by Prometeon Tyre Group and the following SDGs:

## Prometeon Tyre Group supports the Sustainable Development Goals

The Sustainability Plan has been developed in accordance with the 'Value Driver' model drawn up by the  UNGC  and  sets  targets  that  combine  growth,  productivity,  Governance  and  risk  management. Moreover, through the adoption of the Ten Principles of the UNGC and the inspiration for the 17 SDGs,

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

the  Company endeavors to create the perfect correlation between the four main areas of the Ten Principles and the SDGs, where indeed the latter address the former in a more detailed manner.

*Source: Fondazione Global Compact Network Italia

<!-- image -->

In all countries where Prometeon Tyre Group operates with manufacturing plants, local affiliates are assigned with specific targets identified in accordance with Company's sustainability approach. The targets  include  the  constant  monitoring  of  Compliance  with  the  SA8000®  Standard,  the  specific reduction  in  the  accident  frequency  index,  the  specific  reduction  (weighted  on  the  total  finished product) in energy consumption and water withdrawal, as well as control of the sustainability of the supply  chain,  dissemination  and  local  implementation  of  Group  Policies  and  engagement  with stakeholders.

The Report shows the sustainability performance of Prometeon Tyre Group in 2022, compared to 2021. The  information  systems  that  contribute  to  collect  the  data  accounted  in  the  Report  are:  HSE-DM (Health, Safety and Environment Data Management), SAP HR (SAP Human Resources) and SAP/HFM (SAP Financial Accounting).

The Report is structured into four main areas which are in line with the Ten Principles of the UNGC ( see disclosure table pages 273 - 276 ) and directly related to the SDGs as showed in the table above:

- Governance and Business Ethics , an introductory section related to the Sustainable Management Model adopted by the Company, related Policies and activities;

- Economic  Dimension ,  in  which  the  distribution  of  added  value  is  detailed  along  with  the management and performance relating to customers and suppliers;
- Environmental  Dimension ,  which  describes  the  management  of  environmental  aspects  and impacts for the Company plants;
- Social  Dimension ,  which  brings  together  the  paragraphs  dedicated  to  governance  of  industrial relation, welfare, the internal community and the external community.

## GLOBAL REPORTING INITIATIVE

During  2022  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  decided  to  apply  the  Global  Reporting  Initiative  in  its  Social Corporate Sustainability Report. GRI's Materiality Principle provides a framework for identifying the key Environmental, Social, and Economic impacts the organization has based on the nature of its business. It  also  takes  into  consideration  the  impact  organizational  activities  and  decisions  have  on  key stakeholders.

Roberto Righi, Direttore Generale di Prometeon Tyre Group . 'In the coming years we will focus on managing the balance between technological development and sustainability, understood as respect for both the environment and all the communities and people with whom the company works on a daily  basis.  With  the  initiatives  undertaken  since  2021,  Prometeon  is  ready  to  continue  its  path  of sustainable growth'.

Nicolas Marchi, Chief Internal Audit &amp; Sustainability Officer di Prometeon Tyre Group: 'The ESG rating evaluates how the company addresses environmental, social and governance issues, referring to the way it manages and maximizes its non-financial risk. ESG-rated companies are proving that, in many cases, they are better equipped to face future challenges. Furthermore, doing it now, anticipating the times,  means  looking  to  the  future  by  optimizing  and  focusing  investments  on  activities  deemed necessary and concrete'.

## MANAGEMENT MODEL

Responsible  management  by  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  runs  through  the  entire  value  chain.  Every operating unit integrates economic, social and environmental responsibility in its own activity, while cooperating constantly with the other units, implementing the Group strategic guidelines.

The  main  management systems  adopted  include ISO  9001,  IATF  16949,  ISO  14001 and  I SO  45001 certifications. Moreover,  the  Company  is  inspired  by  the  requirements  of  Standard SA8000® as  a reference tool for managing Social Responsibility at its Affiliates and along the supply chain.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

This section of the Annual Report 2022, entitled 'Report on Responsible Manageme nt of the Value Chain',  constitutes  the  'Consolidated  Non -Financial  Disclosure'  of  the  Company  and  explores  the Sustainable Management Model adopted by Prometeon Tyre Group, the governance tools to support maintenance and creation of values, relationships with Stakeholders and related connection with the development of financial, productive, intellectual, human, natural, social and relational capital.

## GOVERNANCE AND BUSINESS ETHICS

## SUSTAINABILITY STRATEGY AND CONTRIBUTION TO SUSTAINABLE VALUE CREATION

The Prometeon Tyre Group business and its strategy integrity and transparency: Prometeon Tyre Group is  a  multiband  manufacturer  of  truck,  bus,  agro  and  OTR  tyres,  distributed  all  over  the  world.  The Company  designs,  develops  and  engineers  products  and  servic es  to  meet  customers'  needs  and expectations, upholding the sustainable values at the heart of its corporate reality and implementing sustainable principles into its business strategy and practices.

## THE ORGANIZATION AND ITS REPORTING PRACTICES

Prometeon Tyre Group S.r.L. is a non-listed limited liability company operating in manufacturing and sales  of  tyres especially  for  industry,  transport  of  goods  and  passengers,  and  AGRO  and  OTR applications, with headquarters based in Milan, Italy and a soc ial capital of € 100.000.000. Prometeon Tyre Group through its majority shareholder is part of Sinochem Holdings Corporation Ltd.

<!-- image -->

Prometeon Tyre Group's Corporate Sustainability Report is published annually. The reporting period extends from 01.01.2022 to  31.12.22  and  it  is  aligned  with  the  reporting  period  of  the  Company's annual financial statements (please refer to Prometeon Tyre Group Financial Annual Report).

As Chief Internal Audit &amp; Sustainability Offices, Nicolas Marchi is the designated contact point for the Corporate Sustainability report and can be reached out via nicolas.marchi@prometeon.com.

Prometeon Tyre Group purchases raw materials from producers in different countries, mostly in Latin America (33%) and Asia (29%). All suppliers are evaluated through a specific selection process including

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

a self -assessment which evaluate their level of Quality, Social and Environmental responsibility and before to start the collaboration they have to sign a specific agreement regarding Natural Sustainable Rubber and a Sustainability agreement. During 2022 the Company worked in order to implement during the first quarter of 2023 a Procurement Policy giving more standards, process and requirements to be followed.  Moreover,  suppliers  working  or  having  business  with  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  are  also evaluated through second-part audits with the aim to verify their level of compliance in Environmental Social and Governance.

Production: Regarding the Production process, all raw materials needed are transported in the 4 plants where the Company operates (Brazil Santo André &amp; Gravatai', Egypt Alexandria, Turley Izmit).

All  plants  are  organized  with  dedicated  areas  such  as:  Inbound  materials  (storage  by  area  for  the different  materials  needed),  production,  quality  control,  outbound  (warehouse  storage  of  finished products  and  management  of  waste  products  such  as  recycling  and  waste  disposal  in  dedicated channels for the correct handling).

Consumption: from the warehouse storage finished products are prepared for delivery to end users and customers (including dealers for Replacement channel as well as Original Equipment manufacturers).

The disposal/recycling processes: at the end of the life cycle  the  tyres are  collected from  different consortium which are engaged to dispose the tyres and/or reuse giving them a second life, becoming playground, sounds barrier, fitness mats ( please refer to Environmental Dimension -ELT ).

## PROMETEON TYRE GROUP'S VALUE CHAIN

<!-- image -->

The  Company's  governance  system  is  driven  by  the  highest  ethical  values,  hence  we  o bserve  the principles of integrity, transparency, honesty and fairness on a daily basis in any relationship with our stakeholders. Pursuing the purpose of manufacturing a safe and high-performance product, we have embraced not only the principle of excellence and competitiveness in the marketplace but also the principle of respecting gender equality, human rights, as well as the realization of sustainable product development that respects the environment and future generations, with a view to eco-friendly growth.

The  Board  of  Directors  of  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  S.r.l.  consists  of  six  Directors,  out  of  which  one Chairman and five Directors, and has full authority over the ordinary and extraordinary management of the Company, including major ESG related decisions.

The members of the board have been appointed by the Quotaholders' meeting held on 28 th April 2022 for three fiscal years and, therefore, until the date of the Quotaholders' meeting that shall be convened to approve the financial statements as of December 31, 2024. The six members all together represent the energy and dynamism of youthness as well as experience and wisdom of maturity; while three members have ages greater than 50 years old, the remaining three members' ages varying between 30 and 50.  Two out of six directors are part of the Company's top management. Four out of six directors hold managerial positions in companies of the relevant chain of control.  The Chairman does not hold any  managerial  position  within  the  Company.  The  other  member  with  particular  competences  in Sustainability is also an active member of the Control Committee of Risk, Sustainability and Corporate Governance for Pirelli.

With reference to the competences of the members of the Board, it has to be highlighted that the General  Manager  of  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  S.r.L.  which  participates  also  actively  to  the  Board  of Directors  (approval  of  financial  statements  report  and  corporate  social  responsibility  report)  has specific competences in sustainability with particular focus on ESG.

The presentation and approval of the Sustainability Report also serves as the main training tool for board members on ESG issues.

Although Prometeon strives to comply with the best practices in terms of corporate governance, not being a listed company, no particular provisions have been implemented so far in relation to gender diversity within the Board of Directors, minorities and stakeholder representation, board evaluation. Moreover, the Board members do not receive any remuneration as a consequence of their office, with the exception of those provided for the positions held within the company. The independence and specific  technical  skills  of  the  candidates  are  not  taken  into  consideration  for  the  purposes  of nomination.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

When called upon to approve the consolidated financial statements, the Board also approves, on a voluntarily basis, the sustainability report. For this reason, the directors receive in a timely manner the sustainability report so that they may revise it and comment prior to its approval.

To ensure that the Board remains continuously informed of critical issues and sustainability topics, an agenda with the relevant item is formed before each Board meeting. Moreover, there is a constant update  of  the  directors  on  the  group's  critical  issues  also  outside  the  Board  meetings  through continuous interaction between executives and directors.

One of the Directors, also serving as General Manager, and reporting to the Board, is empowered with specific powers and supervises the day-byday operations of the Headquarter and of the Company's subsidiaries.

Through the General Manager's activities of supervision, the Board of Directors oversees the activities carried out by Prometeon Tyre Group and its subsidiaries. In addition, the inter-organizational control system in place has been built on communication and common policies and procedures. Thereby, the Company's  subsidiaries  conduct  their  activities  under  continuous  coordin ation  with  Headquarter, without however affecting their autonomy.

The Company has appointed a Board of Statutory Auditors, which consist of three standing statutory auditors and two alternate statutory auditors and an Independent Auditor firm according to applicable provision of law.

Remuneration of the members of the Board is made based on fairness and equality, the two principles that shapes the remuneration strategy of Prometeon Tyre Group applied to all its employees from the most junior employee to the persons covering the highest hierarchical position in the Company. Deep discussion  on  this  topic  can  be  found  in  Social  Dimension  chapter,  under  'Rewards  and  Welfare Paragraph'.

In 2017 the Company adopted an organization model (the 'Organizational Model') in accordance with the principles outlined in Italian Legislative Decree no. 231/2001 (the 'Decree'). The Organizational Model is updated whenever necessary and primarily when the legislature enacts provisions amending the relevant legislation.

The Organizational Model also provides for the establishment of a Supervisory Board, responsible for monitoring the  implementation of the  Organizational Model  and  compliance  and  a  whistleblowing channel.

In this regard, the Company has identified the members of the Statutory Auditors as members of the Supervisory Board and has established a whistleblowing channel which enables the stakeholders to anonymously report any actual or alleged violations of laws and policies.

The  Company  has  implemented  a  Code  of  Conduct  which  Directors,  Auditors,  management,  and employees of Prometeon as well as anyone operating in the name and/or on behalf and/or in the interest of the Company, or that has business dealings with it shall comply with in order to prevent situations that  could  lead  to  unlawful  acts  in  general,  and  in  particular  the  crimes  pursuant  to  the Decree.

As presented under paragraph 'Analysis of Materiality and Identified Risks' describing the impacts of Prometeon Tyre Group's business activities on people and environment, having specific communication channels  with  its  stakeholders,  in  particular  those  externals  (business  partners,  suppliers,  local communities  affected  by  business  activities)  is  of  vital  importance  to  the  Company.  To  this  end, Prometeon  Tyre  Group  avails  of  different  platforms  including  the  Corporate  website,  social  media channels  as  well  as  whistleblowing  channel.  Further  information  can  be  found  in  chapter  'The Relationship  with  Our  Stakeholders'  and  paragraph  'Focus:  Reporting  Proc edure  -  Whistleblowing Policy' discussed under 'Our Policies' chapter.

## MEMBERSHIP ASSOCIATIONS

In line with its inspiration for Sustainable Development Goal 17 'partnerships for the goals' Prometeon Tyre  Group  understands  the  importance  of  collaboration  and  cooperation  with  various  actors  in conducting  its  business  activities.  The  Company  engages  with  various  associations  and  other  nongovernmental  organizations  by  participating  various  projects  and  programs,  seeking  to  promote  in every possible way its commitment for sustainability in economic, social and governance aspects.

The United Nations Global Compact is a non-binding United Nations pact to encourage businesses and firms  worldwide  to  adopt  sustainable  and  socially  responsible  policies,  and  to  report  on  their implementation.  UNGC  is a  voluntary  basis  United  Nations  initiative  that  aims  to  encourage businesses worldwide to adopt sustainable and socially responsible policies . Prometeon Tyre Group participates to webinar and surveys provided by UNGC in order to improve its CSR. The Company report its overall sustainability performance on yearly basis through Communication On Progress to the UNGC. European Tyre &amp; Rubber Manufacturers Association - ETRMA represents the regulatory and related interests of the European tyre and rubber manufacturers at both the European and international level. The  association  is  involved  in  continuous  dialogue  with  various  EU  and  international  institutions, national agencies and other industry sectors. As the voice of tyre and rubber goods producers, activities focus on representing, communicating and promoting the industry to policymakers, as well as providing technical advice for its members.  The activities carried out by the Company in collaboration with the ETRMA  are  discussed  in  detail  under  the  paragraph  ' Focus:  ETRMA -European  Tyre  and  Rubber Manufacturers Association' of the present section.

The European Tyre and Rim Technical Organization - ETRTO is an association open to any manufacturer of tyres, rims or valves and whose manufacturing facilities are located in Europe. As tyres are recognized as making a major contribution to road safety, the association is active in its contacts with national and

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

international organizations and legislative bodies in order to make appropriate recommendations. The related activities focus strictly on technical aspects of tyres, rims and valves as far as fitting and use are concerned. For instance, principal objects are to further align of national standards and ultimately to achieve interchangeability of pneumatic tyres, rims and valves in Europe as far as fitting and use are concerned,  as  well  as  the  development  of  technical  legislation  related;  to  establish  common engineering dimensions, load / pressure characteristics and operational guideline; to promote the free exchange of technical information appertaining to pneumatic tyres, rims and valves.

<!-- image -->

Federazione Gomma Plastica represents the Italian r ubber, electric cable and similar industries, with the aim of promoting any initiative useful regarding the progress of the Industry and protecting its interests. It is an association of companies which are operating in the sectors of production of polymer matrix products, rubber, plastic materials and similar, as identified by the Ateco codes assigned by Confindustria. The association participates, together with Confindustria and all its other Associates, in the  development  process  of  Italian  society,  contributing  to  the  affirmation  of  an  innovative, internationalized, sustainable entrepreneurial system capable to promote economic, social, civil and cultural  growth  of  the  country.  On  delegation  from  Federazione  Gomma  Plastica,  Assogomma represents, the Italian Rubber, Electric Cable and Similar Industry towards the Public Authorities and Administrations, Organizations and Associations in Italy and abroad for the matters related.

<!-- image -->

The members of Lastik Sanayicileri ve İthalatçıları Derneği - LASID (Tyre Manufacturers and Importer's Association) are the main tyre manufacturers and Importers operating in Turkey. The objectives of association are to inform the public on issues related to the sector and products, to carry out activities that will promote correct and conscious tyre use, therefore it aims to act as a bridge between the sector and  institutions,  legislators  and  society  by  addressing  the  difficulties  and  problems  of  the  sector collectively  and  making  suggestions  and  presenting  opinions  before  official  authorities  and  other interested parties on the necessary administrative and legal regulations. Additionally, to follow the sectoral developments in European Union member countries and other countries, to inform the public and its members on these issues. Lastly, the association participate in and contribute to the studies carried out in the country and abroad in order to minimize the possible effects of tire production and use on the environment.

<!-- image -->

YASED  International Investors  Association is the  only  non-governmental  organization  (NGO) representing the international companies operating in Turkey. The mission is to enhance the efficiency and profitability of international companies in the country and the level of attraction of the country for investors by contributing to the improvement of the business and investment environment. It aims to become a leading NGO supporting sustainable growth and development of Turkey with international direct investments creating added value. YASED has an important representation power especially visa vie the government and all public authorities, as its member companies operate in 30 countries and over 15 sectors. The association plays a pioneering role in the establishment of sustainable, predictable and competitive legal regulations and legislations in international standards. Lastly YASED works in cooperation with the public authorities in order to increase global competitive power of the country.

<!-- image -->

Lastik Sanayicileri Dernegi -LASDER (Tyre Manufacturers Association) is established by the main tyre manufacturers of Turkey with the aim to collect, transport and recover end-of-life tyres (ELT) from sellers,  service  providers,  mechanics  and  similar  organizations  organized  throughout  Turkey.  For instance, to represent the leading companies of the tire sector in Turkey to direct the waste sector in the best way and to raise the standards with a transparent and fair management, raising consumer awareness spreading the importance of waste and recycling. LASDER continues to be in contact with international  institutions  and  organizations,  i.e.  Ministry  of  Environment,  Urbanization  and  Climate Change in order to add, remove and make other possible changes to the legislation in the light of the experiences gained from the applications, cooperating with the Ministry in creating the appropriate environment for the implementation of possible developments.

<!-- image -->

Associação Nacional da Indústria de Pneumáticos - ANIP represents the tire and inner tube industry in Brazil, comprising 11 companies and 21 factories and acts in defending the interests of the sector and international  trade.  One  of  these  actions  is  in  defense  against  the  practice  of  dumping  by  some companies that import to Brazil with prices below those practiced in other markets, being considered an activity condemned by the WTO (World Trade Organization). For example, anti-dumping measures applied  by  the  government  for  passenger,  cargo,  motorcycle  and  bicycle  tires.  Moreover,  the

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

association  also  acts  in  identification,  denouncing  import  companies  that  do  not  comply  with  the reverse logistics rules for the collection and disposal of waste tires. ANIP has been working on the National  Program for the  Collection  and  Disposal  of  Waste  Tires  since  1999.  In  2007  ANIP  created Reciclanip, an entity dedicated exclusively to carrying out this work in the country.

The association constructively influences public policy in Brazil and the United States, foreign trade and business management topics are considered.

## AMCHAM

Centro das Indústrias do Estado de São Paulo - CIESP is  a  non-profit  civil  entity,  it  brings  together industrial companies and their parent companies, and associations related to the production sector, as well as companies that have activities directly related to the interests of industry. Its actions are based on the preservation of the general interests of industry and its associates (more than 8,000 associated companies). Topics of interest to the producing classes are studied, debated and taken to the federal, state and municipal authorities. The association supports and give advice the members in the legaladvisory and technical, economic, foreign trade, infrastructure, industrial technology, social responsibility,  sustainable  development,  credit,  and  support  in  research,  fairs,  symposia,  business rounds, courses, agreements.

## FOCUS: ETRMA -EUROPEAN TYRE AND RUBBER MANUFACTURERS ASSOCIATION

The European Tyre &amp; Rubber Manufacturers Association (ETRMA) is an international tyre and rubber industry trade association representing nearly 4.400 companies in the EU which directly employ about 370.000 people. The tyre business volume of ETRMA's corporate members represent 70% of the global sales and 7 out of 10 world leaders in the sector are ETRMA Members. With 86 tyre-manufacturing

plants and 16 R&amp;D centers, ETRMA members have a strong manufacturing and research capacity within both the EU and candidate countries.

ETRMA is the main partner of the EU institutions in the development of new policies on the tyre sector and their proper implementation for the establishment of a sustainable regulatory framework at EU level. The  Association  represents  the regulatory and  related interests of  the  European  tyre manufacturers and cooperates with the legislators to create an enabling environment for a continuous dialogue with EU and international institutions.

Prometeon Tyre Group supports and works with the Association in various activities aimed at raising awareness of the EU institutions and Member Countries on the implementation of market surveillance for monitoring compliance with regulations on the general safety of vehicles and tyres and on energy efficiency. The Company has been present and actively participating to various working groups related to diverse aspects of tyre manufacturing and business: trade, label regulation, CO2 emission, noise emission,  in-vehicle  data  access,  on-board  fuel  consumption  measurement  and  communication, product and industry health and safety.

Prometeon Tyre Group actively supported the Association in drafting and adoption of new EU tyre label regulation in the EU, Turkey, and Israel, which came into effect on 1 May 2021. The new label provides consumers with the possibility to access information about the tyre in the European Product Registry for  Energy  Labelling  (ERPEL)  through  scanning  of  QR  code  on  the  label.  During  2022  the  Company continued to support the Association for advocating further improvements on the label, in particular the inclusion of information about other tyre performances such as abrasion/mileage.

<!-- image -->

Furthermore, in 2022, Prometeon Tyre Group actively supported ETRMA in making its contributions and commitment to the twin transition: Green Deal &amp; Digitalization . The new regulatory framework will focus on the following aspects concerning tyre business:

.

With regard to the reduction of noise limits for tyres Prometeon Tyre Group is committed to develop tyres  with  the  lowest  noise  level.  However,  in  agreement  with  the  ETRMA  position,  the  industry demands more attention should be paid to improving the accuracy of the noise measurement method, which is a crucial point for a reliable policy that benefits the entire noise ecosystem, as no requirements on road pavement noise exist at the moment.

Having recorded a remarkable reduction on noise level in last 15 years, tyres naturally reached their physical limits for improving noise performance. Hence any further improvement in noise emissions will negatively impact other significant tyre performances, for instance rolling resistance, which and wet grip, which are crucial to fuel efficiency and traffic safety purposes. As these latter are the key priorities in achieving ambitious goals for stopping climate change, reducing accidents and fatalities. Before potentially tightening rolling sound limits for tyres, attention should be paid to improving the accuracy of noise measurement method, which is crucial for a reliable regulation that benefits the entire noise ecosystem (recall that no requirements on road pavement noise are existing).

As the automotive industry has experienced an unprecedented digital transformation over the last few years, the tyre, mobility and aftermarket services are being disrupted and reshaped by data. In this emerging and promising market, access to vehicle data is a key enabler of innovation and services provisioning. Being aware of the actual and potential contributions, tyre manufacturers have taken advantage  of  digitalization  to  develop  innovative  services  to  improve  road  safety  and  support decarbonization of transport.

The current technical solutions, to access vehicle data via the Original Equipment connection , makes the vehicle manufacturer a key player concerning the management of data generated by the vehicles of its own brands. Considering that only a regulation can ensure to level the playing field for all mobility stakeholders,  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  offers  its  full  support  to  ETRMA  in  its  call  to  the  European Commission for establishing a sector-specific EU legal framework to access in-vehicle data. Such legal framework would allow enhanced mobility services to be developed and deployed, which are necessary to foster competition and innovation on the nascent mobility services market.

Another important development that the year 2022 saw within the digital transformation in automotive industry is the launch of the Global Data Service Organization for Tyres and Automotive Components

(GDSO)  to  standardize  tyre  data  and  manage  access  to  tyre  information  through  manufacturers' databases  based  on  a  tyre  ID  Unique  Item  Identifier.  Such  system  would  allow  a  secure  and decentralized  access  to  tyre  manufacturers'  databases,  enabling  services  based  on  a  global,  open, standardized and secured data platform, under a robust and transparent governance, thus supporting a sustainable and simplified mobility journey.

Tyre Pressure Monitoring System for commercial vehicles is one of the requirements introduced in last two years. In order to meet this novel requirement, Prometeon Tyre Group collaborates with ETRMA, supporting  the  industry  to  develop  an  integrated  vehicle  monitoring  system.  In  2022  Prometeon extended the number of sizes provided with RFID tag for a digital recognition of tyres and management of carcasses at the end of life of a tyre and Prometeon is developing a proprietary technology for a new generation of tyre sensor to monitor in real time temperature, pressure and other data that can be monitored online.

Another  important  activity  where  the  Company  plays  a  key  role  together  with  ETRMA  is  the development and definition of standards of Vehicle Energy Consumption Calculation Tool (VECTO) , which is essential to guarantee that all Heavy-Duty Vehicles (HDVs) to obtain CO2 certificates, including their OE tyres, as stated by the relevant regulation. Prometeon Tyre Group is committed to comply with these strict certification procedures in order to meet all the requirement of Monitoring and Reporting Regulation as in the near future certified rolling resistance coefficient will also need to be reported and included  in  a  Central  Register  for  Data  on  Heavy  Duty  Vehicles.  The  aim  of  the  creation  of  such  a database is to enable the full market transparency on HDVs' performances about CO2 emissions and fuel  consumption.  The  Company  followed  in  2022  the  extension  of  VECTO  tool  for  bus  and  is  also actively participating to the extension of VECTO tool for trailer applications.

The Company is committed to contribute to a healthy environment. To this end, Prometeon Tyre Group supports    ETRMA's  effort  to  develop  a  better  understanding  of  the  possible  effects  of  particles generated during normal tyre use and wear within the Tyre Industry Project under the supervision of the  World  Business  Council  for  Sustainable  Development  (WBCSD).  Furthermore,  Prometeon  Tyre Group engages in a pro-active way also in the EU Tyre Industry's current work about the development of a new test method to measure the wear level of the tyres in view of a future labelling.

In November 2022, a proposal on new Euro 7 standards to reduce pollutant emissions from vehicles and  improve  air  quality  was  presented  to  European  Commission.  The  proposed  standards  include requirements from manufacturers to reduce the emissions of particulates due to tyre abrasion which is an important source of microplastic in the environment. The European Tyre Industry works UNECE level (United Nations Economic Commission for Europe) and in close cooperation with regulators to develop a reliable and representative test method on abrasion. Following the definition of test method, the abrasion requirements in the Euro 7 proposal can be specified.

## SUSTAINABILITY COMMITTEE

A Sustainability Committee by simple definition is a body that is accountable for the sustainability strategy  and  performance  of  the  business .  Not  only  is  it  a  core  part  of  good  governance  in  any company, its role is also to integrate both business and sustainability priorities so that the Company is able to thrive.

The aim of the committee is to establish a unified view of ESG, increasing understanding of all three aspects,  environmental,  social  and  governance,  and  to  promote  robust  standards  of  corporate governance that integrate all these aspects. This will help the industry to integrate ESG factors into the real estate investment decision-making process. The Environmental, Social and Governance Committee ('ESG  Committee')  purpose  is  to  support  the  Company 's  ongoing  commitment  to  environmental

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

Evolution  of  new  raw  materials  and  new  auxiliary  products  prior  to  their  use  in  order  to  identify potentially critical issues for human health and the environment is one of the absolute priorities for Prometeon  Tyre  Group.  The  assessments  are  made  not  only  on  the  basis  of  restrictive  European regulations concerning the management of hazardous substances, but also standards and know-how currently available internationally (specifications, databases, etc.), thereby fully implementing as stated in the specific 'Health, Safety and Environment' Policy.

The Company collaborates with ETRMA also in establishing and enhancing communications between downstream  users  and  chemical  producers,  activities  specifically  envisaged  by  Regulation  (EC)  no. 1907/2006, also known as 'REACH Regulation'. Through the Chemical Group (CG), which is composed by experts from tyre companies and internal members, ETRMA relays with European Chemicals Agency (ECHA), interacts with Member States' institutions and external consultants on monitoring, studying chemicals used in tyre  industries  and  facing  the  new  chemical  legislation  as  per  classifications  and incoming issues. Thanks to Rubber Chemical Monitoring Program, the Association anticipates future actions on chemicals and monitors the substances under evaluation by ECHA and those that can be restricted. In this context, since 2020 the activity of ETRMA had been mainly focused on following the main topics of 6PPD (organic chemical used as an antiozonant in rubber tyres), Cobalt Salts, Resorcinol, Carbon Black, DCBS (sulfonamide accelerator with excellent anti-scorching property and delayed onset of cure).ETRMA is also active to enable a free market supporting the elimination of some local and promoting  the  adoption  of  a  harmonized  certification  frame  to  reduce  the  actual  burden  of administrative procedures in many countries hopefully following UNECE framework.

## ANALYSIS OF MATERIALITY AND IDENTIFIED RISKS

For  the  reporting  of  the  year  ending  31  December  2022,  the  Materiality  Analysis  was  updated  in accordance with the new requirements of the GRI 2021 Standard. The identification of materiality issues for the company is linked, according to the Standard's requirements, to the identification of current and potential significant impacts generated or likely to be generated by the company on the economy,  the  environment  and  people,  including  impacts  on  human  rights,  through  all  of  the organisation's  activities  and  business  relationships.    These  impacts  include  negative  and  positive impacts,  short-term  and  long-term  impacts,  intentional  and  unintentional  impacts,  reversible  and irreversible impacts.

The  dedicated  working  group  set  up  at  corporate  level,  in  collaboration  with  a  team  of  external professionals to support it, structured the materiality analysis process in four different phases:

- Understanding the context in which the Group operates : analysis of the sector in which Prometeon  operates,  taking  into  consideration  different  categories  of  sources,  both internal and external to the company.
- Identification of Prometeon's actual and potential impacts : actual impacts are impacts that have occurred over time, while potential impacts could occur in the future.
- Evaluation of the significance and materiality of impacts : all impacts have been assessed through a process of evaluation of the degree of significance developed in line with the criteria indicated by the Standard.
- Prioritisation of the most significant impacts for reporting purposes : the most significant impacts  for  Prometeon  were  prioritised  and  the  impacts  considered  most  relevant guided the identification of the material issues included in this Sustainability Report.

Below  is  a  list  of  the  themes  found  to  be  material  following  the  identification  and  aggregation  of material impacts. Each material theme has been associated with the relevant GRI Disclosures and, for each theme, the appropriate reporting boundary has been defined.

| Material Topics  Impacts  Positive/Negative  Actual/Potential  Significance  Energy  consumption and  the fight against  climate change  Negative impacts on climate  change  Negative  Actual  Very relevant  Increased energy  consumption  Negative  Actual  Very relevant  Management of  pollutant  emissions  Impact of air pollution on  health and the environment  Negative  Actual  Very relevant  Waste  management and  circular economy  Health and environmental  impacts of waste  Negative  Potential  Relevant  Climate change mitigation  through waste utilisation  Positive  Potential  Relevant  Use of sustainable  materials and  Responsible  procurement  Deterioration of raw  materials  Negative  Actual  Very relevant  Protection of  water resources  Increased water stress  Negative  Actual  Relevant  Protection and  development of  human capital  Lack of worker protection for  non-trasparent recruitment  practices  Negative  Potential  Relevant  Human capital development  Positive  Actual  Relevant   |

In general, an impact may be actual or potential, negative or positive, short or long-term, intentional or unintentional, reversible or irreversible.

Actual impacts are those that have already occurred, while potential impacts are those that may occur but, at the time of analysis, have not yet occurred.

The assessment of each positive impact has considered Prometeon's direct and indirect contribution to the Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs) in line with the commitment made and taking into account the  management  tools  put  in  place  to  monitor  the  targets  set.  With  regard  to  the  assessment  of potential  negative  impacts,  all  policies,  procedures  and  activities  implemented  by  the  company  to prevent and mitigate the identified negative impact were considered.

The prioritisation activity allowed the company to determine the material issues for reporting. At the methodological level, the main negative and positive impacts identified were prioritised and evaluated according to their degree of severity and likelihood of occurrence, respectively. The significance of an actual  negative  impact  is  determined  by  its  severity  while,  the  significance  of  a  potential  negative impact is determined by the severity and likelihood of the impact.

Specifically, the severity of an impact was assessed by taking into consideration three aspects:

1. Scale :  how  severe  the  impact  is  and  the  external  context  in  which  the  impact  occurs, including geography;
2. Scope : how widespread it is and can be measured in terms of its impact on the value chain;

3. Irremediable character 1 : how difficult it is to repair the damage generated by the impact.

## IMPACTS

Prometeon Tyre Group develops and manufactures premium tyres for consumers and customers who value safety, sustainability, and innovative products. Sustainability is at the core of Prometeon Tyre Group  business  and  one  of  the five  cornerstones  of  the  Company's  strategy. Management  of sustainability  at  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  is  based  on  its  values:  focusing  on  balancing  technological development and sustainability, understood as meaning respect both for the environment and for all the communities and people. Through sustainable business practices, Prometeon Tyre Group can offer safety and security to all stakeholders, e.g. employees, customers, local communities, shareholders, the environment, our suppliers, and local governments. Prometeon Tyre Group business is guided by the ethical  principles  presented  in  the  Board  approved  Code  of  Conduct.  The  document  specifies  the principles for  Prometeon Tyre Group business, including instructions for various matters related to ethics and anti-bribery guidelines. Prometeon Tyre Group does not condone any form of bribery within the Company's operations.

Every employee needs to adhere to the Code, and Prometeon Tyre Group has online training for all personnel. The majority of employees conducted the training in 2019 and the training is included in the induction for new employees. Moreover in 2023 the Company will provide a new training regarding Code  of  Ethics  and  Conduct.  The  company's  sustainability  activities are  led  by  Internal  Audit  and Sustainability  Department,  who  is  a  member  of the  Group's  Management  Team. The  Group's Sustainability Group supervises and monitors the sustainability work within the Group and comprises Supply Chain, Research &amp; Development, Products &amp; Innovations, Finance, Human Resources, Health Safety and Environment, Manufacturing, Marketing, Compliance and Communications. The Manufacturing supervises and monitors the progress in reducing greenhouse gas emissions. The duties of all supervisors include day-today leadership of Sustainability.

## ENVIRONMENTAL IMPACTS

Reducing emissions according to the targets settled, improving energy efficiency, improving the rolling resistance of tyres Carbon dioxide, CO2, is the most significant greenhouse gas generated by traffic. Fuel consumption during driving is the single most significant environmental impact over a tyre's service life and approximately 89% of a tyre's life  cycle,  CO2  emissions,  are  generated  during  driving  in  case  of  combustion  engine  vehicles. Lowering the rolling resistance of tyres means less fuel and thus less CO2 emissions. For electric vehicles lower rolling resistance means longer driving range and lower energy use.

1 Irremediability character was considered only for the assessment of negative impacts as indicated by the GRI 2021 Standard.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

By the end of 2019, Prometeon Tyre Group had reduced the rolling resistance of its tyre.  Prometeon Tyre Group is fighting the effects of climate change and reducing emissions according to targets defined by  improving  energy  efficiency,  increasing  the  proportion  of  renewable  energy  in  production,  and encouraging  raw  material  suppliers  and  logistics  partners  to  reduce  their  emissions.  Achieving greenhouse gas reduction targets (scope 1 + scope 2) are part of the Sustainability plan of Prometeon Tyre Group. Moreover, thanks to the Headquarter Sustainability Committee, the team is setting target for reduction till 2030 and at the beginning of 2023 the Company will monitor its consumption through a specific software that will be able to help the Company to highlight the criticalities and apply action plans to reduce every year the greenhouse gas emissions.

With reference to the waste management the Company is committed to reduce waste. During 2022 the Headquarter  Committee  worked  to  set  target  for  waste  reduction  until  2030.  Moreover  in  2021 Prometeon Tyre Group obtained Sustainability linked loans, the financing formula includes a pricing linked to the achievement of sustainability objectives by Prometeon and is intended for the refinancing of existing debt and business needs, including new investments. In line with the "Sustainability-Linked Financing Framework", funding pricing is linked to the Group's sustainable policies: Prometeon has always been attentive to these issues and active in many initiatives linked to the commitments made with  its  Social,  Environmental,  Economic  and  Governance  Policies,  within  the  Strategic  Model  of Sustainable Growth adopted. To have access to this refinancing activity the Company set targets and KPI regarding Environmental Dimension (tyre waste goals), Social Dimension (training organized by HR) and Economic Dimension (third party audits on suppliers) that need to be reached before the end of 2025. (please refer to Economic Dimension -Sustainability Linked Loans -targets ). The Company work on continuous improvement of the sustainability of raw materials in tyres, zero waste to landfill from production and promoting the circular economy.  Regarding scrap tyres, or tyres that do not meet high standards  of  quality,  are  taken  to  recycling  or  sold  to  other  providers  that  use  the  scrap  as  a  raw material in their production. The Company promotes the recycling of end-of-life tyres together with its stakeholders in all the Countries where the Company operates with production plants and commercial offices ( please refers to Environmental Dimension -End of Life Tyres ). Prometeon Tyre Group goal is to increase the share of renewable and recycled raw materials in tyres. To achieve this goal, the Company is  in  close  cooperation with different raw material manufacturers, Universities for the study of new sustainable  raw  materials  and  encourages  innovations  from  different  stakeholders.  Some  of  the innovations for sustainable raw materials are already used in the tyres produced by Prometeon Tyre Group.  ( Please  for  further  details  refers  to  paragraphs  'Biodiversity'  and  'New  Materials'  in  the Environmental Dimension and to 'Prod uct Safety, Performance and EcoSustainability' in the Economic Dimension ). Prometeon Tyre Group is committed to safeguarding natural resources. The company's aim is to create tyres that are both safe and more environmentally friendly, to find renewable and recycled raw materials and to replace fossil raw materials. Prometeon Tyre Group also works to avoid the  use  of  harmful  substances,  thereby  minimizing  risks  to  occupational  health  in  production  and warehousing. One promise in the Health Safety and Environment Policy and in the Sustainable Natural

Rubber  Policy  is  to  continuously  improve  the  environmental  friendliness  of  products.  The  most significant environmental impacts of the plants where the Company operated come from waste and energy consumption. The utilization rate of the production waste has been growing for years, and Prometeon Tyre Group reports the amounts of waste as well as energy consumption annually in the Corporate Sustainability Report. Concerning the protection of water resources, Prometeon Tyre Group with the support of the Risk Management Committee is able to monitors and take actions to mitigate the risks related to water management and the protection of water resources in all the plants where the Company operates. Moreover, as per waste targets, the Headquarter Committee defined target to be reached in 2030 to reduce water consumption in its production operations. It has to be highlighted that Prometeon already works to preserve the water resources using different types of resources of water for its Production process and water used is also reused for other needs (watering plants and garden in the factories, please for further details, refers to Environmental Dimension -Water )

## SOCIAL IMPACTS

Safety and well-being of personnel, diversity, inclusion, and equality in the workplace. Prometeon Tyre Group safeguard the development of human capital, respect human and workers' rights, advance an inclusive and equal working culture and empower people to be active members in the  organization.  Active  participation  at  different  levels  of  the  organization  and  respecting  every employee's right of participation are parts of Prometeon Tyre Group working culture. Prometeon Tyre Group wants to empower people to actively participate and collaborate, to take both ownership of their work and responsibility for everyone's safety. The Company goal is to minimize the number of occupational accidents and promote occupational health. Prometeon Tyre Group is working toward a workplace with zero accidents. Prometeon Tyre Group respects human rights and treats all individuals equally.  The  company follows the wellbeing  at  work  and  outside  giving  to  all  employees'  services provided by welfare.  The Company reports the activities in the Corporate Sustainability Report ( please refers to Social Dimension -Internal Community). Prometeon Tyre Group occupational health care is based on local legislation and on its Health Safety and Environment Policy. In all factories, located in Brazil, Egypt and Turkey, occupational health is supported and managed by local Health, Safety and Environment team.  All factory locations organize regular monitoring of working conditions to identify potential risks  to  the  health  of  workers  as  discussed  in  detail  under  paragraph 'Health,  Safety and Environment' paragraph in Social Dimension chapter. All  employees are also offered the service of medical assistance and medical consultations. In 2021 and 2022, to support communities in the fight against the spread of the COVID-19 pandemic, the Company has organized onsite vaccination clinics in all  its  factory  locations  and  in  dedicated  hospital  for  people  working  in  offices.  Moreover,  the vaccination  campaign  has  been  extended  to  all  families  of  Prometeon  Tyre  Group's  employees.
