## NEW MATERIALS

Nowadays manufacturing companies give attention to systems that reduce and eventually eliminate waste and manage raw materials scarcity through the continual use of resources. In other words, the focus  is  on  design  by  developing  tyres  for  both  longevity  and  optimal  performance  in  terms  of sustainability, on continuous innovation to improve production processes by employing new and/or alternative  raw  materials  which  sustainably  reducing  waste,  energy  consumption,  gas  emission, material loss, recovering waste-streams and, where technically possible, replacing materials that may challenge significantly the material recycling of the tyres.

The concept goes through along the whole supply chain, including purchasing of raw materials to the end-of-life  of  the  products.  The  key  is  extending  product  lifetime  and  closing  material  loops  by implementing sustainable approach with 3Rs concept -Recycle, Reduce and Rethink &amp; Reinvent.

As far as longevity is concerned, the focus is to design and develop tyres in the ways that facilitate repairment  and  retreading,  thus  increasing  tyre  lifetime  and  reducing  environmental  impact  not compromising tyres performance in terms of safety and efficiency. Attention has to be put also on the type of materials used in production, therefore focusing on circularity of the materials which means exploring new potential sustainable raw materials, which can be reused and recycled, to replace the actual ones aiming to create fully sustainable products.

With  these  challenges  lie  ahead,  assessment  and  innovative  concepts  together  with  constructive cooperation and development would be necessary along the whole tyre supply chain in order to foster know-how exchange with professionals, developing the new innovative strategies with stakeholders in line  with  the  market  trend  and  evolution.  In  these  circumstances,  the  focus  of  the  strategies  is  on

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

durability, maintenance, repairability and upgradability, i.e. circularity of the whole process, aiming to increase products longevity in terms of performance and products utilization rate, as well as to improve material recycling and energy recovery, therefore to design and produce fully sustainable products as mentioned, aiming to protect human health and reduce environmental impact.

Following this purpose, the Company is introducing new materials in tyres formulations that have lower impact on environment. For example, by using silica obtained from rice husk ash to replace the classic silica produced from quartz sand. Rice husk ash is one of the most common agricultural waste; it is an agricultural waste by product from rice milling in the rice production. Moreover, processes which allow to recover energy by burning this waste have been developed. Additionally, from purification of ashes obtained, it is possible to get excellent silica in terms of purity which can be used in the compounds replacing the classic filler materials.

Another key element in the journey into sustainability is the replacement of raw materials deriving from petroleum with bio-based renewable raw materials. For instance, the Company is reducing the use of oils and resins deriving from petroleum and replacing them with ingredients of natural origin such as soybean oil and pine resin. In this way, new product line with high percentage of sustainable compound and materials has been designed and developed; it will be launched in the next future.

On top of that particular focus is put on one of the main components of the tyres as well  - Natural Rubber. Natural Rubber is a completely renewable material, and it is mainly produced only in certain areas  of  the  planet,  equatorial  zone.  Prometeon  is  committed  the  conservation  of  forests  and biodiversity  by  collaborating  with  suppliers  which  distribute  worldwide  internationally,  reducing therefore the impact in some areas of the world (Malaysia, Indonesia, Thailand) where natural rubber is normally bought. In addition, the Company has adopted "Local 4 Local" supply chain policy which is focused on local sourcing and shorter supply chains in order to reduce pollution from raw materials transportation and the CO2 footprint.

Over and above that, another element which would be crucial and has to be sustainably managed is tyre waste as it is not possible to erase totally the generation of tyre waste during the production. For instance, an idea has to be put in this field aiming to improve the production processes performance, prevent waste generation and keep it as low as possible, consequently reducing also the materials to be disposed. In other words, it may impact positively economic and environmental point of view.

<!-- image -->

## END-OF-LIFE TYRES

Prometeon  Tyre  Group  is  strongly  committed  to  create  sustainable  life  cycle  of  tyres,  developing therefore innovative and sustainable approach in which the goal is to reduce resource waste as well as recover as much as possible energy and materials during their life cycle.

Tyres that are no longer sufficiently safe or efficient to be reused, even if retreaded, are referred as 'end -oflife tyres' (here in after referred as ELTs) which are practically wastes. ELTs can pose a serious threat to the environment and have to be collected and recovered with specific methods, as the rubber of tyre is made of a mix of very high-quality polymers with exceptional chemical-physical characteristics which remain unaltered even in the recycled rubber obtained from it. It is possible to channel ELTs towards one of two different processes: they can be either recycled, a process where it is possible to obtain new materials or recovered as energy.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

As  a  tyre  manufacturing  company,  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  has  a  particular  sensibility  for  the environment. The Company pays great attention to both the sustainability of all production processes along the entire value chain and to the product disposal and recyclability.  In line with its inspiration for SDG ' 9 Industry, Innovation and Infrastructure ', ' 12 Responsible Consumption and Production ', ' 17 Partnerships for the Goals ',  together  with  local  initiative  which  collaborates with, Prometeon Tyre Group  is  committed  to  produce  tyres  that  can  have  a  'second  life'.  To  this  end,  the  Company cooperates strictly with suppliers who produce raw materials from ELTs, such as micronized reclaim rubber and recovered carbon black, as well as with organizations dedicated to ELT recovery. Thanks to the Company's tyre f ormulations containing these materials and the goal to increase their presence as much as possible, Prometeon Tyre Group continues to offer high performance tyres in terms both of quality  and  safety  at  the  same  time.  Moreover,  the  use  of  these  materials  can  provide  for  an opportunity  to  contribute  to  the  creation  of  a  circular  economy  as  well  as  reduction  of  the  CO2 emissions.

Prometeon Tyre Group has always been at the forefront of innovation and now, with sustainability being a clear focus, even more so developing new materials from recycling of End-of-Life Tyres (ELT) and production waste stream to deliver product performance with enhanced CO2 emission reductions.

Conscious of its joint responsibility towards a sound management of ELT, Prometeon Tyre Group has recently  decided  to  make  a  step  forward  and  to  adopt  a  more  pro-active  approach  regarding  ELT management compliance in Italy. As a result, following a thorough market survey, Prometeon Tyre Group partnered with an Italian company that is an active participant to the United Nations Global Compact likewise Prometeon Tyre Group.

Through this new collaboration, starting from January 2023 Prometeon Tyre Group will actively monitor its ELT management compliance operations in Italy, through real-time updates enabled by the advanced ICT platform developed by the service partner, providing an enhanced and more responsive level of service to the market. Similarly, Prometeon Tyre Group will be able to enhance and further focus its R&amp;D  efforts  in  the  areas  of  sustainable  materials  from  ELT  and  overall  life-cycle  sustainability. Moreover, the materials  that  could  be  recovered  and  reused  in  production  thanks  to  the  new  ELT management would promise a positive contribution to the Company's GHG emission indexes rela ted to Scope 3 as well as to finishes and semi-finished product waste indexes.

Thanks to the collaborations with various companies and NGOs around the world, it is possible to reuse the tyres by reconstructing the outer belt - provided that the carcass is still intact -or by means of specific production processes the rubber is crumbled and transformed into different products/materials that can be used in different fields: bio-composites, urban heating materials, sound barriers,  flexible  cement  grout,  playgrounds,  acoustic  underlay,  athletics  tracks,  steelworks,  fitness mats,  tennis  courts,  asphalt.  Furthermore,  ELTs  can  also  be  recovered  as  energy  at  ad  hoc  plants.

Indeed, they have the same calorific value as coal, but they cause much lower amounts of climatechanging emissions.

In relation to this, in Brazil , the Company collaborates with ReciclANIP, which during the course of 2022 recycled a total of 443.296 tons of ELTs from various tyre producers operating in the Country. About 13% (58.022 tons) of the total amount of ELTs recycled in the Country was provided by Prometeon Tyre Group Brazil.

In Argentina where Prometeon Tyre Group has a large and structured commercial presence through a network of 120 sales points, the Company continued to its collaboration started in 2021 with Geocycle, a waste management company operating at worldwide level in the field of recovering and recycling industrial, agricultural and municipal wastes. During 2022, to promote a better management of ELTs, the Company supported its dealers through commercial rebates for sending their ELTs to Geocycle's waste collection centers for recovery and recycle. Thanks to collaboration, in 2022 about 530 tons of ELTs were recovered as energy used in cement production. Prometeon Tyre Group's collaboration with external partners for ELT management in Argentina is not limited to the above mentioned one. In 2022 the Company started to work also with Regomax,a company producing rubber crumbles from ELTs.

In Turkey , the Company is member of LASDER , association founded by tyre manufacturers operating in the Country for the collection and recycling of ELT. Thanks to the collaboration and willingness of tyre manufacturers  in  the  Country,  the  total  amount  of  ELTs  recycled  in  2022  equals  to  approximately 204.655 5 tons,  of  which  26.116  (approximately  12,8%  of  the  total  amount)  tons  were  provided  by Prometeon Tyre Group Turkey.

The  above-described  collaborations  are  not  just  the  simple  proof  of  Prometeon  Tyre  Groups environmental sensibility, they also reflect the Company's commitment to environmental communication, which today is an essential element for companies in giving a concrete answer to the word ' environmental sustainability '.

## EXPENSES AND INVESTMENTS

Prometeon  embraces  the  ten  fundamental  principles  of  the  UNGC  and  supports  the  Sustainable Development Goals defined by United Nations during the 2030 agenda, aiming to achieve a better and more  sustainable  future.  Inspirited  by  aforementioned,  budget  and  investments  are  planned,  the Company has set a 5-years road map to further extend and develop its sustainable approach.

As part of an ambitious plan, the Company increases its investment in Sustainability for the next years. In relation to Manufacturing the investment plan is dedicated in the following areas:

- -Technology &amp; Quality;
- -Energy &amp; Sustainability;
- -Health, Safety &amp; Environment.

Regarding  Technology  &amp;  Quality,  it  is  related  to  direct  reduction  of  waste,  the  improvement  and development regarding the use of new materials, factory processes and high-quality machinery. It is aiming to reduce production waste as much as possible and to have better control process, therefore, to improve product quality and performance as well.

As  far  as  Energy  &amp;  Sustainability  is  concerned,  investment  will  be  in  place  in  order  to  reduce consumption of energy, steam, water and consumables such as polyethylene by saving any kind of production resources.

With reference to Health, Safety &amp; Environment, the focus is on the improvement of plants safety, working  environment  as  well  as  reduction  of  pollution/emissions.  It  has  set  therefore  zero  work accidents, better  working conditions and high sustainable approach from social and environmental point of view as target.

<!-- image -->

The focus is not only on production side, the Company pays great attention also on other aspects aiming to extend fully the sustainable approach. As explained in Social Dimension, considering also the core of Sustainability,  Prometeon  puts  effort  and  spends  many  resources  in  sustainable  initiatives  and development by paying attention on internal and external community therefore the whole chain of the business as well as promoting sustainable culture in the way the Company believes.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

## QUALITY -FROM VISION TO MISSION TO ROAD MAP

For  Prometeon,  Quality  is  the  base  of  Global  Footprint  Industrial  strategy.  Our  vision  is  perfectly represented by Prometeon logo and slogan 'Smart and Sustainable'. A quality approach based on digital innovation and sustainability. This vision is embodied within each pillar of Quality with a circular approach; where everyone is involved and integrated in the activities in a matrix structure.

<!-- image -->

Then transversally the vision has been cascaded into mission for each of the pillars through the Quality.

This  acts  as  a  powerful  tool  giving  directions,  and  most  important  purpose,  for  accomplishing organizational  objectives.  Ensuring  implementation  through  a  company  mindset;  to  enable  quality excellence through our value chain.

- Supplier  Quality  Pillar: aims  for  an  advanced  supplier  performance  evaluation  and  supplier social accountability;
- Compound  Quality  Pillar: aims  for  an  advanced  compound  properties  evaluation  and compound scrap or rework improvement;
- Process Quality Pillar: aims  for  process  live  management  and  digitalization,  and  to  improve waste and recycle;
- Product Quality Pillar: aims  for  tyre  performance  linked  to  the  production  flow,  and  rolling resistance predictive analysis;
- Outgoing Quality Pillar: aims  for  automatic  defect  recognition  and  finished  product  rework improvement.

From  the  statements  relating  to  smart  and  sustainability,  our  roadmap  was  born.  Defining  short, medium and long terms projects to reach our objectives.

The introduction (to be implemented between 2022 and 2024) of the New fully integrated Quality Management System Platform, on a WW base, is a crucial milestone towards the development of our Smart and Sustainable Quality strategy, the New Platform is an integrated environment covering the whole value stream, complying with the latest standards.

## Quality Week 2022

From 7-11 November 2022, Prometeon celebrated Quality globally with focus on ' Quality means doing it right when no one is looking ', that in fact implies a quality conscience, quality as a value and a proper mindset that Prometeon has to promote.

## ECONOMIC DIMENSION

## THE DISTRIBUTION OF VALUE FOR OUR STAKEHOLDERS SHARING OF ADDED VALUE

In  line  with  inspiration  for  the  SDG  '8  Decent  Work  and  Economic  Growth',  the  Ethical  Code  of Prometeon Tyre Group ratifies  the  commitment of the  Company to operate to ensure responsible development over the long term, while being aware the bonds and interactions that apply to economic, social and environmental dimensions. This is to wed the creation of value, progress of the Company, the attention given to stakeholders and raising standards of living and quality of the environment.

Added value means the wealth created over a given reporting period, calculated as the difference between the revenues generated and the external costs sustained in the period. Distribution of added value  among  stakeholders  allows  the  relations  between  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  and  its  main stakeholders to be expressed by focusing attention on the socio-economic system in which the Group operates.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

The total generated value in 2022 recorded a significant increase about 39% compared to 2021 mainly due to the growth in production value - equal to 39% as well -between the two years. It has to be highlighted that this growth in production value is driven by revenues generated by net sales, which increased by 45% compared to 2021.

In  parallel  with  the  total  generated  value,  the  total  distributed  value  in  2022  recorded  a  similar performance. Indeed, the distributed value increased by 40% compared to 2021, mainly due to growth in value distributed to the community and operating costs.

For  further  in-depth  study  on  trends  in  the  items  determining  generated,  distributed  and  retained values shown above reference should be made to the Consolidated Financial Statements of Prometeon Tyre Group.

The change in the items determining gross global added value as shown above, are set out in the Consolidated Financial Statements section of this report, to which the reader is referred for further details.

Prometeon is committed to its ' Local 4 Local" supply chain policy focused on local sourcing and shorter supply chains for reducing CO2 footprint caused by transportation as well as contributing to economic and  social  development  of  local  communities  where  the  Company  has  significant  operations. 'Significant operations' means production, marketing, sales, distribution of tyres and R&amp;D activities considered all together. The Company's significant operations are located in the Countries where the production  pl ants  are  present,  i.e.  Brazil,  Egypt  and  Turkey.  "Local  communities'  instead  refers  to people living and carrying out business activities in the mentioned countries. All the documents related to policy and procedure practices that need to be applied by Procurement Department and with reference to the specific social and environmental issues covered, the Company undertakes to establish and maintain the procedures necessary to evaluate and select its suppliers on the basis of their level of quality, social and environmental responsibility, as well as to request their suppliers to implement a similar management model, in order to strengthen responsible management in the supply chain that extends beyond its direct supplier. In line with its 'High Value' devel opment strategy, the Company does not limit its assessments of products and services offered to the mere cost proposed, paying utmost attention instead to the value proposed, thereby including quality, innovation, flexibility, economic, social and environmental responsibility and business ethics. Starting from the very first contact, all Prometeon Tyre Group suppliers were and are assessed on their social, environmental and business ethics responsibilities in addition to the economic and products or services quality to be supplied.  In  the  final  stage  every  purchase  order  is  'contractually  bound'  to  the  Sustainability  and Business Ethics Clauses.

The self-assessment procedure introduced in 2018 requires to new suppliers of raw material and high value added (HVA) parts, to auto-certify the fundamental requirements of sustainability performance with respect to the principal national and international regulations on work, environment, and business ethics. Moreover, it has to be noted that the self-assessment provided to all suppliers, analyze and select these latest considering the ESG (Environment, Social and Governance) requirements.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

Moreover, in 2022, in order to verify that suppliers result compliant with the fundamental requirements regarding the social, environmental and business ethics responsibilities, Prometeon Tyre Group decided to continue with audits on site with the collaboration of a second-party audit company . Procurement Department together with Internal Audit &amp; Sustainability Department selected a sample of suppliers including all types: natural rubber, chemicals, carbon black etc. Furthermore, with the issuance of the Policy  on  Conflict  Minerals  and  Cobalt  in  2021,  the  Company  decided  to  audit  in  2022  suppliers susceptible to conflict minerals and cobalt to verify the correctness of the procedure applied by the supplier to sell these latest to Company ( please refer to Governance and Business Ethics of this Report -Conflict Minerals and Cobalt Policy ). Regarding the results of the audit, for all suppliers who have not fulfilled  all  the  required  standards,  action  plans  are  defined  with  expiration  dates  which  are  then verified on site and need to be approved with evidences by the auditor of the second party.

Based on the results obtained during the audit carried out in 2022, it emerged that 33% of suppliers have  been  identified  as  having  significant  actual  and  potential  negative  social  impacts  with  which improvements were agreed and signed by the suppliers concerned upon the result of the assessment carried out by the second party audit company.

During 2022 100% of all new suppliers having collaboration with Prometeon Tyre Group have been evaluated in the area of Environment, Social and Governance. During 2022 18 audits of suppliers of raw materials (natural rubber, chemicals, carbon black, cobalt and minerals) have been verified (covering the  20% of the total  spending equal to 640 MEuro) by second party audit company to attest their compliance in terms of social standards and impacts. Furthermore, Prometeon Tyre Group due to the nature of the raw materials and geographical area where these latest are purchased, all of these 18 audit and suppliers can have significant actual and potential negative social impacts.

Moreover, it has to be noted that during the course of the year, 0% of suppliers were terminated due to actual and potential negative social impact as a result of the assessment.

Here below the summary of the result of the audits carried out during 2022 used to evaluate the overall performances of the suppliers of raw materials in term of sustainability:

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

<!-- image -->

Prometeon Tyre Group defined the following criteria for the results of the assessment:

- &lt; 50 % → termination of the supplier;
- 51 % &lt; 70 % → action plan agreed and signed with suppliers (deadline 30, 60, 90 days);
- 80 % → suppliers selected for future supplier award that will be defined before the end of 2023.

Due to the countries where the Company operates and to monitor the operations at significant risk of child labor, Prometeon Tyre Group through the Risk Management Committee, which monitors in its significant locations of operation and at general level, in all the countries where the Company operates, that child labor is not practiced in any case and in any type of operation (such as manufacturing plant). Moreover, to monitor the entire value chain and due to the geographical area where raw materials are purchased,  the  Company  through  the  above-mentioned  second  party  audit  monitors  also  that  the prohibition of child labor is fully respected by its supplier and the related location of operation where they operate. Furthermore, in line with its commitment to the UNGC and its " Principle 1: Support and respect  the  protection  of  internationally  proclaimed  human  rights '  and  ' Principle  2:  Ensure  that business practices are not complicit in human rights abuses '; and the application of the policies (Global Human Rights  Policy,  Conflict  Minerals  and  Cobalt  Sourcing  Policy,    Social  Responsibility  Policy  for Occupational Health, Safety and Rights, and Environment) publicly available on the official website Prometeon Tyre Group contributes to the effective abolition of child labor.

Prometeon Tyre Group with the support of the Risk Management Committee and through the second party  audit  carried  out  for  the  suppliers  monitors  operations  and  suppliers  at  significant  risk  for incidents  of  forced  or  compulsory  labor.  Due  to  the  type  of  operation,  the  Countries  where  the Company operates and where raw materials are purchased, Prometeon Tyre Group takes measures to contribute to the elimination of all forms of forced or compulsory labor. Moreover, respecting ' Principle 3: Uphold the freedom of association and the effective recognition of the right to collective bargaining ' the Company verifies that all its workers and those of the suppliers can have the possibility to prevail over  their  rights  (Global  Human  Rights  Policy,  Conflict  Minerals  and  Cobalt  Sourcing  Policy,  Social Responsibility Policy for Occupational Health, Safety and Rights, and Environment).

The  procedure  is  reviewed  by  the  Internal  Audit  and  Sustainability  Department  and  based  on  the answers given to the questionnaire decides whether to approve the supplier or not. In cases where the supplier  results  to  be  not  compliant  (even  if  issues  not  material)  with  Prometeon  Tyre  Groups sustainability requirements, the department adopts action plans which will be communicated to the supplier in order to implement the corrective action and to fill up the gap with areas of improvement identified.

The Company agreed to reach specific target to obtain Sustainability linked loan. For this purpose, goals have been set with a 5-year plan which will involve 88% of its Raw materials supplier base. Here below the audit plan shared and approved:

In 2022 the Company overachieved the targets and commitment for sustainability audits, strengthening the position of Prometeon Tyre Group.

With regard to the contractual stage, for ten years now, the Sustainability and Business Ethics Clauses (including anticorruption) have been included systematically in contracts and orders for the purchase of goods and/or services and/or works, both with private suppliers and with the Public Administration (or institutes/enterprises under public control), and in agreements with NGOs, worldwide.

In particular, the clauses require awareness on the part of suppliers of the principles, commitments and values contained in Prometeon Tyre Groups Sustainability documents, being the 'Ethical Code'; the 'Code of Conduct', the 'Global Human Rights' Policy, the 'Health, Safety and Environment' Policy, the 'Anti -Corruption  Program',  published  and  accessible  on  the  web,  clarifying  the  principles  of  the

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

Company in its  activities  and  relations  with  third  parties,  contractually  and  otherwise  require  that suppliers confirm their commitment to:

- not using or supporting the use of child labor and forced labor;
- ensuring equal opportunity, freedom of association and promotion of the development of each individual;
- opposing the use of corporal punishment, mental or physical coercion, or verbal abuse;
- complying with the laws and industry standards concerning working hours and ensuring that waves are sufficient to cover the basic needs of personnel;
- not tolerating any type or bribery in any form or manner and in any legal jurisdiction, even where such practices are effectively permitted, tolerated, or not subject to prosecution;
- assessing and reducing the environmental impact of their own products and services throughout their entire life cycle;
- using resources responsibly with the aim of achieving sustainable development in compliance with the principles of respect for the environment and the rights of future generations;
- establishing and maintaining the necessary procedures to evaluate and select suppliers and subsuppliers on the basis of their commitments to social and environmental responsibility, regular overseeing compliance with this obligation on the part of the same;
- specifying that the Company reserves the right to verify at any time through activities of audit, either directly or through third parties, that fulfillment of the duties taken on by a supplier has been achieved.

The Sustainability Clauses have been translated in different languages, where the suppliers operate, to ensure maximum clarity and transparency vis à vis a supplier in the matter of the contract duties that they assume, not only in respect of the Company itself, but also at their own site in relations with their own suppliers.

From  the  standpoint  of  utmost  assurance,  suppliers  of  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  have  available  the Whistleblowing Reporting Procedure (please refer to the Governance &amp; Business Ethics section of this report to have more details about the platform launched By Prometeon to support stakeholders being available  24/24  and  7/7  (https://prometeontyregroup.whistlelink.com/),  which  has  been  indicated expressly in the clauses and by means of which any breach or suspected breach they discern in relations with the Company referring to the contents of the following can be reported in total confidentiality: the  'Ethical  Code',  the  'Code  of  Conduct',  the  Company  policies  'Global  Human  Rights',  'Health, Safety and Environment', 'Anti -Corruption Program'.

In  2022 Prometeon Tyre Group showed resilience and innovative approach in difficult times due to pandemic making the brand stronger and safeguarded for years to come. The Company is committed to strengthen the sustainability initiatives extending the sustainability audits to raw materials, capex and services suppliers, applying self-assessment model and the audits on site. Moreover, during 2022

the Company worked to draft a sustainable procurement policy which will be issued in 2023 and will be applicable to all types of suppliers. To recognize the suppliers' commitment, the Company worked t o set up a Supplier Award. The suppliers will be evaluated on the basis of different KPIs in which one will be related of sustainability-ESG project. Detailed information on the topic will be available in the next corporate social responsibility report of 2023.Prometeon Tyre Groups relationship with suppliers and external collaborators is funded on business integrity and respect for equal opportunities for all the subjects involved in the purchasing processes, as required by the 'Ethical Code'.

Several Prometeon Tyre Group policies highlights not just the full integration of the sustainable supply chain but also the Company's already present commitment in addressing the causes underlying the SDGs ' 8 Decent Work and Economic Growth ' and ' 12 Responsible Consumption and Production ', as most  of  the  policies  have  been  adopted  and  implemented  before  adherence  to  the  UNGC  and inspiration for the SDGs. The sustainable management of the supply chain is also the subject of integral part of the 'Social Responsibility for Occupational Health, Safety and Rights and Environment' Policy, the  'Global  Health,  Safety  and  Environment'  Policy,  the  'Global  Human  Rights' Policy, the  'Global Quality' Policy, the 'Sustainable Natural Rubber' Policy.

- Quality and System certification ISO 9001: In 2019, Prometeon Tyre Group had sustained the certificate  with  successful  surveillance  external  party  audits  for  Head  Quarter  and  all  the operative units and in 2020 the Company had the first re-certification from external party audit. In 2021 and 2022, the certificate was sustained with successful surveillance external party audits for  Head Quarter and all our oper ative  units,  the  positive  results  confirming previous  years' trend of for the entire Prometeon Tyre Group;
- IATF  16949:2016 :  Following  the  evolution  of  the  ISO  9001:2015  standard  and  the  new  IATF 16949:2016 (Automotive Scheme that became private), Prometeon Tyre Group in 2018 has achieved the certification of the Quality Management System. Prometeon Tyre Group is one of the leaders in the achievement of these updated significant and demanding certifications. In 2019, the Group has sustained the certificate with successful surveillance external audits. In 2020 has passed successfully the first Re-certification from external audit and on 2021 and 2022, has sustained the certificate with successful surveillance external party audits, which confirms the robust Quality Management Systems in-place and the continuous improvement approach

Starting from the results, action plans were assigned for areas, also defining the improvement to reach interdependent level of maturity.

## LINK WITH THE TERRITORY PRODUCT SAFETY, PERFORMANCE AND ECO-SUSTAINABILITY

In line with its inspiration for the SDGs ' 9 Industry, Innovation and Infrastructure ' and ' 12 Responsible Consumption and Production ' Prometeon Tyre Group is committed to develop alternative production site and materials with low impact on the environment, i.e. materials with no ingredients of petroleum origins and those recycled. Product safety and performance, combined with the sustainability goals, is the main focus of Company's product research and development activities.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

adapted in Prometeon Tyre Group with 2 minor non-conformities assigned in 2022, an 80% reduction regarding the period 2017-2019 yearly average.

Prometeon Tyre Group is a company aimed at excellence, efficiency, transparency and technological innovation that develops, produces and sells tyres for Medium and Heavy Commercial Vehicles for good and people transport, Agriculture and OTR. Prometeon's multi -level offering with a product portfolio that includes the PIRELLI, FORMULA, PHAROS, ANTEO, SESTANTE, ERACLE, TEGRYS brands.

The  company  has  developed  its  own  technological  know-how,  implementing  and  improving  the licensed technology of Pirelli Tyre SpA.

With over 100 years of experience in the commercial tyre industry, offers a complete range of products and services for all types of segments.

Research and Development expenses for the Prometeon Tyre Group for the year 2022 amounted to 26.7 million euros, of which 7.3 million are made for investments.

The Company has 3 R&amp;D centers, one in Brazil, one in Italy and one in Turkey, and 1 Development Center in Egypt, which allow the autonomous development of products that meet the requests from the various markets of the main geographical areas of the world. Of these R&amp;D centers, the newest one was opened in Izmit Turkey in 2020, with the new Materials Laboratory opened in 2021.

'P rometeon Tyre Group opens a new R&amp;D center in Turkey'

<!-- image -->

Prometeon Tyre Group R&amp;D is based on an Open Innovation approach to develop new knowledge through collaboration with Universities, Startups, and Companies that have consolidated know-how in

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

specific areas of study. Thanks to projects realized jointly, the Company can develop new technological knowledge to improve the performance of commercial vehicle tyres in terms of safety, sustainability, energy saving and environmental impact.

Prometeon Tyre Group's Research and Innovation activities respond to these ma rket needs and to the necessary focus on sustainability in which the Company is paying a lot of attention.

All the thematic developed are aimed at the enhancement of the fundamentals (Research) and to the innovation of product performance through:

- the study and acquisition of know-how;
- creation of virtual models;
- use of new laboratory survey methodologies;
- new instruments for measuring and reproducing the phenomenon;

and thus, for the identification of a possible innovative solution and its consequent introduction in the products produced by the Company.

The main role of R&amp;D Department is to develop new products and processes through innovations, which are based on the following 6 pillars:

- collaboration with lawmakers in order to gather information regarding legislation evolution both at national and international level;
- and  from  business  point  of  view  with OEMs in  order  to  understand  and  analyze  the development and growth regarding products and technologies, therefore aiming to study analyze and anticipate future market trends;
- technology  evolution  of  the  product to  be  up  to  date  with  the  latest  developments  in Innovation;
- application of digital  mobility  solutions to  tyre  business  in  order  to  increase  the  overall safety and performance;
- open innovation to foster collaborations with experts in their relevant fields for increasing the Company know how;
- and carrying out all the above-mentioned  activities paying a great attention to sustainability , i.e. respecting to the nature, caring for the society and ensuring a continuous innovation.

<!-- image -->

During the last years, several projects have been started with a particular focus on the study of new sustainable materials, the recovery of waste and the waste materials coming from different working cycles.  In  this  regard,  several  collaborations  have  been  agreed  with  universities  that  represent excellence in different fields of research.

The  recent  focus  of  the  industry  for  the  digitization  and  development  of  IOT  systems  has  recently highlighted  the  need  to  collect  and  process  information  from  tyre  usage  enabling  through  the implementation  of  algorithms,  the  best  use  of  both  in  terms  of  environmental  impact  (energy consumption reduction) and safety, allowing a constant and real-time monitoring of conditions of the use of tyres. The optimization of their use and related maintenance can lead to fuel efficiency and longer life, reducing fuel consumption and significantly increasing the life of the tyres with a consequent reduction of the impact on the environment.

The push given by the European Community requiring a substantial reduction in CO2 emissions, has led to  an  increase  of  restrictive  regulations  for  vehicle  manufacturers.  In  fact,  the  limits  set  by  the  EU legislation  envisage  a  reduction  in  CO2  emissions  of  15%  by  2025  and  30%  by  2030  (accordingly Prometeon Tyre Group defined its own targets as discussed in Environmental Dimension). The major vehicle  manufacturers  have  required  to  all  their  component  suppliers  to  perform  improvement activities to reduce energy consumption and thus for all components used in their vehicles. It has to be noted  that  tyre  manufacturers  have  been  firstly  involved  and  required  to  optimize  energy-saving performance without penalizing other essential qualities such as safety and durability.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

Moreover, several national entities have promoted incentive actions in the use of low environmental impact vehicles and components, giving a greater push for research and innovation on new materials, new technological solutions, new production processes, new control systems.

Thanks to the focus on sustainable innovation and technological solutions developed in the Research and Development centers of the Company, the new 02 Series of Prometeon is developed based on two key words, Innovation and Sustainability, which represents the maximum in terms of cost efficiency for professionals in the transport sector and respect for the environment.

Compared to previous product lines, the design effort has been managed with an efficient management of operating costs to ensure, on one hand high mileage and on the other and an "active sustainability": longer tyre life and lower rolling resistance, which is the results of a decrease in tyre consumption and fuel consumption.

The development activity of the new lines has been focused on formulations containing sustainable materials, on innovative technological solutions and processes that have led to a significant reduction in the weight of tyres, to obtain, with equal safety standards, a very balanced product, able to offer the best performance in full compliance with the principles of sustainability.

In  May  2022,  Prometeon  launched  5  new  product  lines  focused  on  energy  saving  (Profuel)  and increasing the durability of products maintaining the same performance. In particular, the new H02 Profuel Series tends to extreme the energy efficiency of the tyre (rrc &lt; 4.0 N/kN - &gt; Class A), these latest are dedicated to all sector of use where long-haul driving at a constant speed are able to maximize the positive effect of reduction allowing the CO2 reduction target imposed on heavy vehicles that need to be reached by 2030 (- 30%).

Based on calculations made with VECTO (the simulation tool developed by the European Commission and used to determine CO2 emissions and fuel consumption of heavy vehicles) a trailer equipped with 385/55 R 22.5 H02 PROFUEL Steer tyres and 315/70 R 22.5 H02 PROFUEL Drive on the tractor, and 385/55 R 22.5 on the semi-trailer, consumes 1.4 liters less per 100 kilometers than a vehicle equipped with the previous product line.For a fleet of 150 lorries that each cover 130 thousand kilometers per year, means 702 tons of lower CO2 emissions and a fuel cost saving of 465 thousand euro per year (average price of diesel fuel of 1.7 €/l).

In order to force at is maximum the reduction o f  the tyre's rolling resistance, the R&amp;D Department decided to proceed applying combined action of more or less decisive factors but also acting on the type of materials used, optimizing the structure and the overall reduction of tyre weight.

For the structure cords made of high strength steel were used giving high flexibility and preventing the spread of corrosion, ensuring the integrity and longevity of the casing. Zero-degree spiral belt cords with new geometry and increased diameter for durability and strength.

Reinforced heel, in combination with the new geometry of the metal wires that gives great flexibility, strength and high load capacity. Innovative belt pack configuration to optimize tyre weight, contain energy dissipation, promoting low rolling resistance and fuel consumption containment.

Using engineering systems for numerical calculation and through virtual simulations it has been learned that the tread affects more than 50% of the rolling resistance coefficient coming from material used and from the drawing. However, to achieve these very challenging goals, the Company has completely redesigned the tyre by re-engineering all the materials inside the tyre as per compounds and structure.

Thanks to the know-how of the designers, an innovative concept of tread design, which is a Prometon's patent, has been developed. The aim of this design was to reduce the mobility of the various parts of the  design  itself  maintaining  adherence  qualities  on  slippery  surfaces  and  thus  thanks  to  the contribution of innovative materials that maximize road holding while maintaining very low levels of hysteresis, that is low energy consumption during the rolling.

Through an optimized combination of materials and tread pattern, mileage performance has also been preserved on long-haul applications, optimizing tyre life efficiency. This has been possible using a high percentage of silica in the tread compound which required necessarily a new production process of the tyre be able to discharge the electrostatic charges integrating in the tread a conductive insert.

The development of the new Profuel product lines required four years of study, 180 million kilometers of tests on the roads in Italy, Germany and Turkey. 2,500 tires have been tested with a working group of  one  hundred  professionals  involved  in  the  project  composed  by  Research  and  Development, Marketing, Logistics and Commercial Department. Thanks to the results obtained with the tests on wet, dry and snow, the Profuel attained the winter homologation 3PMSF. Moreover, it has to be highlighted that mileage and structural strength tests were conducted, in line with current product lines, as well as all tests related to the required legal approvals.

In addition, all 02 Series tyres are equipped with an internal RFID sensor that allows identification.

The sizes and products offered by the line H02 Profuel are the following:

- 315/70R22.5 156/154M H02 Profuel Steer;
- 315/70R22.5 156/154M H02 Profuel Drive;
- 385/55R22.5 160/158L H02 Profuel Steer.

## Product line dedicated to electric vehicles and urban buses

The aim of the project was the development of a new line of products dedicated for the equipment of latest  generation  of  electric  traction  vehicles  specialized  in  transport  of  people  in  urban  areas.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

Sustainable mobility, especially in urban contexts, is a theme driving to the decision to renew and/or upgrade the fleet vehicles that are used for urban public transport at worldwide level:

' Within seven years, nearly half of the city buses in service worldwide are expected to become electric. Therefore, the total number of electric buses is expected to be more than triple, from 386,000 in 2017 to about 1.2 million in 2025, accounting for about 47% of all urban bus fleets worldwide. Major cities are moving in the same direction: Paris, London, Milan, Mexico City and Los Angeles are among the 40 cities that have committed to investing only in zero emission transport by 2025 '. (Source: European Transport Forum 2019).

The main manufacturers of heavy vehicles are releasing on the market new generation electric traction buses that meet a twofold objective: to increase operating autonomy by using more powerful batteries, but  of  a  heavier  weight,  and  maintain  the  same  passenger  capacity  as  conventional  and/or  hybrid vehicles and thus lead to an increase of the overall mass of the vehicle.

To  meet  these  needs,  tyre  manufacturers  were  required  to  develop  new  products/measures  with increased load capacities (which were not allowed by international standards at the beginning of the study) and reduced rolling resistance, to limit battery charging cycles.

Starting from the knowledge acquired regarding the advancement of the latest generation of products, the  development  and  design  activity  has  been  focused  on  the  research  of  combinations  for technological solutions properly balanced that could achieve the main performance targets required.

U02 URBAN-e PRO Multiaxle meets the needs of the new generation of electric mobility vehicles: thanks to an increased load capacity, compared to the previous product line, that allows the use of batteries with high autonomy without compromising the number of passengers.

The new product line has a rolling resistance reduced by 10% compared to the previous product and is classified among the best in class with Category B on fuel consumption. U02 URBAN-e PRO Multiaxle helps increase the autonomy of city buses, reduce battery charging cycles for fully electric vehicles and reduce CO2 emissions.

In line with the evolution of hybrid and electric vehicles for urban use, characterized by frequent stop and go, the new product reduces operating costs, as well as offering a higher level of comfort, a uniform wear and reduced generation of noise (69dB in measure 275/70R22.5) and thus with excellent safety performance.

U02 URBAN-e PRO Multiaxle is suitable for all axles. The M+S and 3PMSF markings, together with the Category B in wet grip, ensure a high level of performance even in winter conditions as well. To achieve this performance, Prometeon R&amp;D has redesigned materials and internal structures of the product. The

introduction of the new SWITe technology and the reinforced structure have allowed an increase in load capacity, while improving rebuildability of tyres. To increase the durability of the product subject to strong creeps and impacts against sidewalks, a reinforced sidewall profile and a new compound more resistant to abrasion have been added. In order to optimize the rolling resistance performance, the design criteria of the newly developed tread patterns, with reduced deformability, were used, applying the concepts contained in the patent application filed.

Such drawing installations shall be such as to permit compaction by limiting the mobility/deformation of the longitudinal grooves or transverse anchors in particular loading or driving conditions. Limitation of the deflection energy of the tread contributes to reducing the hysteresis of the tyre. However, in order to keep in line the wet sealing performance with the previous product, a particular geometry of the longitudinal grooves that guarantees an enlargement of the cross-section of the same at mid-life promoting  water  drainage  and  therefore  vehicle  controllability,  has  been  adopted  (see  patent IT201700084726A1).

Furthermore, the new tread geometry has been designed to ensure excellent impact resistance and high traction throughout the life cycle, taking into account also the high torque transmitted by electric traction engines on tyres. Prometeon has developed this product line in collaboration with the main manufacturers of new generation of urban vehicles working with electric motors or alternative fuels to diesel.

In particular, Prometeon Tyre Group in collaboration with the company E-CO Hev, which produces an innovative electric vehicle without batteries but with a super condenser, is continuing a project on sustainable  urban  transport  in  major  Italian  metropolitan  cities.  The  new  line  is  available  in  the following sizes:

- 275/70 R 22.5 152/148J with Category B in fuel consumption and wet grip, Category A (69dB) in external rolling noise;
- 315/60 R 22.5 156/150J with Category B (best in market class) in fuel consumption and wet grip, Category A (72dB) in external rolling noise.

## R02 PROFUEL for motorway, regional and urban routes

Also for the Regional line, that is, the versatile product for the equipment of trucks with tyres with 17.5" and  19.5"  shrinkage  that  operate  on  motorway  routes,  regional  and  urban,  the  same  concept  of reducing fuel/energy consumption and increasing durability was pursued. The tread patterns have been designed to provide grip and traction in all seasons and on wet and moderately snowy surfaces, as well as to ensure the optimal distribution of the footprint on the ground for the benefit of uniformity of wear and mileage.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

The new geometry of the sides and the increase in the thickness of the rubberization, measurable with ISW  indicator,  give  resistance  to  impacts  and  contribute  to  the  longer  life  of  the  products.  The performance improvements of R02 PROFUEL compared to the previous product line are as follows:

- -20% rolling resistance;
- +15% acoustic comfort;
- +10% grip on wet and snow;
- +10% wear regularity.

During 2022, the entire R02 Profuel product line was completed, covering the main market measures in 17.5" and 19.5" shrink sizes

Sizes 285/70 R 19.5 and 225/70 R 17.5 are among the best in class of premium brands for European tyre labelling values.

Based on calculations made with VECTO, the R02 PROFUEL 285/70 R 19.5 tires used to equip a 4 2 vehicle allow a fuel saving of 0.8 liters per 100 kilometers compared to the previous product line, which result (for a fleet of 50 vehicles with a distance of 130 thousand kilometers per year each and an average price of diesel fuel of 1.7 €/l) in 40 tons of lower CO2 emissions and a saving of 88 thousand € per year in fuel costs.

## 6 Pillars for Innovation

*6 Pillars of Iinnovation of Prometeon Tyre Group

<!-- image -->

## ROAD SAFETY

As being the fundamental pillar of transport systems, safety is top priority. As mobility continues to grow and is radically transformed by digitization (the process of converting information from a physical format  into  a  digital  one),  decarbonization  (reduction  or  removal  of  carbon  dioxide  from  energy sources) and innovation, the opportunities to further improve safety performance must be seized.

Synergies between safety and sustainability measures should be exploited. For example, encouraging the use of zero-emission modes of transport, new and safer forms of mobility may also go hand-in-hand with improved access to mobility for all members of society.

Mobility is the cornerstone of freedom of movement of people and goods, which is fundamental to the smooth functioning of the society. Transformation that mobility is going through is not limited only to industry itself, but it changes our lives by increasing the competitiveness of the industry, which results in outputs aimed at making mobility more accessible, jobs safer, and ensuring safer, cleaner and more sustainable transportation services.

On  the  other  hand,  the  pursuit  of  such  goals  leads  to  radical  changes  in  the  mobility  landscape, disrupting conventional transport business models and industries, bringing not just new opportunities but also new challenges. The commitment of Prometeon Tyre Group is to spread road safety culture through training, awareness-raising campaigns and innovative technological solutions for sustainable mobility.

## HIGH VALUE APPROACH TO FUTURE MOBILITY

Prometeon Tyre Group carefully monitors the evolution of the automotive market and more generally the evolution of mobility. Future mobility trends such as digitalization, electrification, management of shared transport and driving automation, are gaining more and more momentum and will lead to an evolution of the mobility sector whose speed is unprecedented in the sector. The Company constantly studies the opportunities brought by the future mobility trends, and in line with its inspiration for SDGs ' 8 Decent Work and Economic Growth ' ' 9 Industry, Innovation and Infrastructure ', ' 11 Sustainable Cities  and  Communities ',  ' 12  Responsible  Consumption  and  Production ',  develops  and  evaluates projects that can enable Prometeon Tyre Group to create a difference in the global effort for making our world sustainable.

## OUR CUSTOMERS

Prometeon  Tyre  Group  is  the only  global  tyre  manufacturer  entirely  dedicated  to  the  Industrial market , which includes truck, bus, agro and OTR tyres .

## Sales channels include:

- Original Equipment , addressed to vehicle manufacturers;
- Replacement , for the substitution of tyres on vehicles already in circulation.

As part of Replacement , there are two broad types of customers (truck and bus segment): on one side the trade operators (specialized dealers and distributors) and on the other side the fleets (end-users) which can be either supplied directly or indirectly through dealers.

Specialized resellers are tyre specialists operating on the market in the role of independent businesses; specialized dealers constitute a fundamental point of contact between Prometeon Tyre Group and an important part of the end-user (fleet) market. Particular attention is devoted to specialized dealers in terms of shared development, to enhance the product offering integrated with a high-quality level of services,  for  instance,  tyre  management  and  monitoring,  breakdown  service,  casing  management, retreading.

" Distributors " are partners who are fundamental to guarantee continuity in the supply of tyres to other specialized and non-specialized resellers. They do so by offering local delivery and distribution services throughout the entire territory.

Fleets can have various dimensions, depending on the number of vehicles; in the smallest one, the fleet owner is driving his own vehicle; the largest ones have more than 500 vehicles.

Original Equipment Customers require to compile specific sustainability assessment to evaluate the corporate social responsibility of the provider, in this case Prometeon. During the years the request augmented  and  the  requirement  to  be  compliant  have  been  more  and  more  strict  and  precise. Moreover, Original Equipment customers require also that the Company present targets and KPI to be defined  for  the  year  2025 -2030  (please  refer  to  Governance  &amp;  Business  Ethics -Headquarter Committee section of this report for Prometeon Environment KPI / Targets). All customers require to Prometeon to furnish documentation regarding audit carried out on value chain, sustainability clauses and standard that need to be signed by the supplier in the contract agreement to verify that all the standards have been respected in the entire value chain.

## SAFE MOBILITY: PRO CHECK

In 2022 Prometeon Tyre Group continued to offer its clients the best solutions in terms services through PRO CHECK , application developed by the Company for effective fleet management. This latest is the first fully developed in-house tyre inspection and monitoring system .

<!-- image -->

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

Prometeon Tyre Group supports its clients by providing them with the best digital solution to regularly inspect and monitor their tyres. By being sure that tyres are always at their best, both for air pressure and residual tread depth, fleets can lower the risk of breakdown and, consequently, increase the overall driving safety. PRO CHECK provides fleets and dealers with an easy-to-use inspection system, supported by a smartphone app and a measuring tool, and ever-reliable online and offline detailed reporting about tyres  and  vehicles  status.  Starting  from  the  data  entered  by  the  customer  systematically  (e.g.  tyre pressure, tread depth, odometer value etc.) the system runs specific data analytics algorithms to detect abnormal operational conditions that might lead to early tyre wear and possible risks for driving. This helps to alert the customer on time, about any possible problem, giving them a chance to prevent safety issues.

Moreover, the forecasting will also allow fleet customers to carry out predictive maintenance actions , optimizing vehicle routes and stops and helping them to save fuel and reduce CO2 emissions . Future functionalities in PRO CHECK will involve a focus on a real-time data collection system. As the amount of collected data will grow, Prometeon Tyre Group will be able to adapt and design better forecast algorithms.

It  has  to  be  highlighted  that  the  main  benefit  in  terms  of  environmental  sustainability  is  the considerable increase in tyre life - which will contribute positively to fuel-saving and pollution reduction - and reliability. This will allow to predict when the tyre will be completely depleted as well as develop in the future new kind of related services.

Since 2021, the service has been available in Europe (Italy, UK, Germany, Spain, Poland) and in Brazil.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

*Pro check services

Smart solutions are becoming the core on the tyre market. Prometeon Tyre Group puts effort and innovates constantly and consistently in this field in order to build an ad hoc and tailor-made solution for customers professionals, thus, to create a whole smart and digital solution package of which can meet perfectly and satisfy the needs of the market. In the coming future, the Company is preparing and looking forward to launch and make it available.

## THE IMPORTANCE OF NETWORKING

SuperTruck Network is made up of 90 clients which 33 are members of SuperTruck Consortium in Italy. The 'networking' among the business community is the key in strument for Trade Marketing to support the sell-out multi-brand retails rather than a simple sell-in strategy. Especially in business-to-business

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

markets like the one of industrial tyres, networking plays a fundamental role in supporting for a real enhancement of the offer made to end-users in terms of product and services. Trade marketing and product sell-out are two concepts that linked to each other indissolubly. On the one hand, a true sellout action on the end-user requires an available, strong and coherent Network, while on the other hand the success of networking activities relies on an ever greater direct and indirect sell-out action. After the successful implementation of the SUPERTRUCK network in Italy in 2019, 2021 was a year of further consolidation of the Network in Europe. During 2022 the new president of the Super Truck has been presented  to  the  network,  the  role  has  been  assigned  to  the  Chief  Financial  Officer  of  Europe  of Prometeon.

## PROJECT OF THE MUNICIPALITY OF MILAN TO BUILD AN EXEMPLARY SMART MOBILITY DISTRUCT IN TERMS OF SUSTAINABILITY AND INNOVATION

As  far  as  SDGs  ' 11  Sustainable  Cities  and  Communities ',' 17  Partnerships  for  the  Goals '  are concerned, Prometeon Tyre Group is committed to make every possible contribution aimed at making sustainable everyday life. To this end in late 2019, Prometeon Tyre Group has joined a specific task group formed by the Municipality of Milan, 5 other companies and 2 public entities which have shown interest an d have been involved in the initiative with the aim of transforming Bicocca District, the City's business district.

252 The participants are Prometeon Tyre Group, prestigious public entities and multinational corporations such as Deutsche Bank, Engie, ING, Prysmian Group, Pirelli, the University of Milano-Bicocca and the

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

National Research Council of Italy ( Consiglio Nazionale delle Ricerche ), all located in Bicocca District and willing to take part in this innovative and ambitious initiative aimed at transforming the District into an exemplary Smart Mobility District, and to promote the sustainable mobility to all employees of the companies. The main topics examined and studied in 2021 were discussed during virtual roundtables due to the Covid19 pandemic. At the heart of the initiative lays the implementation of sustainable and innovative transport solutions in the district such as the promotion of the use of public transport giving the possibility of having more stops for buses that pass in the area, the adoption of alternative transport systems such as car and bike sharing stations.

During 2022 the project related to the Smart Mobility District continued, several meetings have been organized to discuss about the next steps for the current year and the topic discussed during the round table were the following:

<!-- image -->

- ❖ Presence at work and  average days of smart working in each Company participating to the round table have been discussed. It emerged that Prometeon Tyre Group is aligned with the other companies of the district, employees apply 2 days of smartworking per week;
- Software  Movesion :  is  a  company  that  operates  in  the  field  of  sustainable  mobility  and corporate welfare. For years it has been a leader in mobility management through the provision of services and technological solutions to the largest companies in the country. Together with the other companies it has been decided to fill out a questionnaire (mobility managers will be responsible to input the data related to the Company and to the headcount and employees will compile the forms giving information of their living places and means of transportation used between their homes and workplaces). When the questionnaire will be filled out also by the employees it will be possible to define the new workplace mobility. During 2023 a meeting will

be scheduled with AMAT - Agenzia Mobilità Ambiente e Territorio and Movesion to understand how to define the workplace mobility of the District of Bicocca;

- Public Transport : during 2022 the round table of Bicocca District discussed about the possibility to  require  to  have  discount  on  annually  subscription  without  reaching  a  total  number  of adhesions to obtain the 20% of discount. It has been considered to offer the discount through an  external  provider  and  for  this  reason  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  can  consider  to  offer  this discount with the support of Eden red (provider of ticket restaurant and discount on selected partners/brands) and the welfare system;
- Area B : it has been confirmed that Bicocca District is not involved in the new application of the AREA B in Milan which is an area of restriction that has been put in place in 2022 with the aim to improve the overall air quality in Milan;
- Bus line 87 : the new bus stop confirmed that employees can better reach the offices in the area thank to stop in via Pirelli crossing via Della Strada Bianca;
- Hybrid  and  plug  in  car :  in  2022  it  has  been  discussed  how  it  is  important  to  sensitize  the employees to choose an hybrid or plug in car;
- Carpooling service : Carpooling (or car pooling) is one of the aspects of sustainable mobility and the sharing economy that is increasingly gaining ground in the habits of Italians regarding travel. Carpooling is simply the sharing of the car with other people who have to travel the same route, the benefits are many such as fuel savings, but above all a lower environmental impact and an improvement in traffic congestion. The carpooling provides that a driver makes available the free seats in his car with other passengers who must travel on the same route, dividing the travel costs. In Italy, the legislation not only allows this practice, but even encourages the service offer to people working in the same area to take only one car to reach the office and this with the aim to save Co2 emissions and reduce environmental impact. At the end of 2022 the working group decided to fix a meeting with the dedicated provider who furnished a detailed application on the innovation will be that the working will ask to have access to all employees working in Bicocca District, not only Prometeon Tyre Group.

## PROMETEON TYRE GROUP AND THE KPI RELATED TO SUSTAINABILITY LINKED LOAN OF EUR 350 MILLION

The  financing  formula  includes  a  pricing  linked  to  the  achievement  of  sustainability  objectives  by Prometeon and is intended for the refinancing of  existing debt  and  business  needs,  including new investments.

In line with the "Sustainability-Linked Financ ing Framework", funding pricing is linked to the Group's sustainable policies: Prometeon has always been attentive to these issues and active in many initiatives linked to the commitments made with its Social, Environmental, Economic and Governance Policies, within the Strategic Model of Sustainable Growth adopted.

To have access to this refinancing activity the Company set targets and KPI regarding Environmental Dimension (tyre waste goals), Social Dimension (training organized by HR) and Economic Dimension (second party audits on suppliers) that need to be reached before the end of 2025.

During 2022 the Company has been assessed by an external audit assurance company to confirm the bank the declaration of the results for the baseline 2021 and for the first result carried out for the year 2022.

<!-- image -->

The data and information reported in this Report refer to the Group (defined as the Company and each of its Subsidiaries) for the year ended on 31 December 2021.

The methodology and criteria used to  calculate  each  of  the  three  Sustainability  KPIs  are  described below.

## KPI 1 - Scrap tyre waste

Defined, for any calendar year, as the ratio between the tyre waste of the Group , expressed in terms of Total Scrap Cost (€) , and the total direct and indirect cost of production of the Group factories (' CAF' or 'Costo a fabbrica' or 'Total Standard CAF Value' ), expressed in €. The ratio is then expressed as a percentage.

Definitions:

Total Standard CAF Value: refers to the total direct and indirect cost of production of the factories ( namely Costo a fabbrica or CAF ), expressed in €. The total production value is obtained by adding the values of all Finished Products items delivered to the warehouse. The value of each Finished Product item is obtained by multiplying the quantities delivered in the warehouse (Kg) by its Standard Cost (€ / Kg). A specific internal procedure (Prometeon Procedure 10.07 for 'Management of Quality Costs') has been developed for the calculation of the Standard Costs.

Tyre Waste refer to:

- Semifinished Components Waste :  components  to  the  Finished  Product  that  fail  the  quality checks before being assembled and are therefore classified as scrap. Such components can be made up of Compounds, Textile materials, Metallic materials, Beads and Covers and the scrap are expressed in kg, weighing each of them.
- Finished Product Scrap : Finished Product Visible Scrap (including X Ray) and Finished Product Tyre Uniformity Scrap (Force, Unbalance and Geometry), both detected by factory inspection and by warehouse audits, expressed in kg. Such scraps are expressed in kg, weighing each of them.

Each Semifinished Component / Finished Product has its own Standard Cost (expressed in € / kg) which depends on the quantity and type of raw materials used, the manpower needed to produce it and the expenses (e.g. energy) and is fixed by the Controller Function through the Management Plan which is defined each year. The cost of waste for each Semifinished Component / Finished Product is then valued as the multiplication of th e quantity scrapped (Kg) for the relative Standard Cost (€ / Kg). By summing the values (€) of all the Semifinished Components / Finished Products scrapped, the Total Scrap Cost (€) is obtained.

No limitation in the calculation of the KPI are applied.

## KPI 2 - Spending on Suppliers second party audit

Defined,  for  any  calendar  year,  as  the  ratio  between  the Cumulative  Spending (expressed  in  €) associated  to  raw  material  (natural  rubber,  chemicals,  carbon  black,  cobalt)  suppliers  audited in

terms of sustainability by a third-party sustainability auditor regarding that calendar year and the Total Spending on direct raw material suppliers in year 2020 , determined at € 620 million.

In the calculations of this KPI, indirect suppliers and suppliers from the offices are excluded.

## KPI 3 -Number of employees trained

Defined, for any calendar year, as the ratio between the number of trained employees of the Group and the Group headcount at year-end . The ratio is then expressed as a percentage.

## Definitions :

Number of trained employees: Total number of employees who attended, for each country/region, at least 1 training during the reporting year.

The training in scope does not include mandatory courses, but soft training, technical courses, training linked to "sustainability" topic. The training modalities can be in presence, virtual, hybrid, asynchronous, synchronous.

Group headcount at year-end : Total number of employees active as of 31 December of each reporting year (including white collars and blue collars for each country/region). External workers and interns are not included. Employees that change Classification or Region during the Year, are counted as per their allocation on 31 December.

In the calculations of this KPI, APAC employees trained are excluded. 2022 Results

At the moment of the preparation the present corporate social responsibility reports the external audit assurance company has not yet confirmed the data declared here below by Prometeon Tyre Group. The official results revised showed that regarding training and procurement the Company overachieved the targets and for the waste the target has not been reached as expected due to some reorganization in the manufacturing area in 2022. Moreover, result regarding 2022 will be assured and will be publicly available on the report of the next year.

## ESG RATING ASSESSMENT

The assessment has been carried out analysing the CSR Report 2021 approved by Board of Director and published on the official website of Prometeon Tyre Group;

The annual Rating results obtained covers all the Sustainability KPI such as:

- Governances &amp; Business Ethics;
- Social Dimension;
- Environmental Dimension;

- Economic Dimension.

The methodology applied by Sustainalytics is based on an evaluation considering a range from 0 to 100. More  the  Company  is  closer  to  0  and  more  the  'overall  unmanagement  risk'  is  lower,  more  the performance is considered good.

The overall result obtained by Prometeon Tyre Group is 14,9 showing a low risk exposure ('overall unmanage ment risk') in terms of ESG and with a consequent strong ESG risk Management .

## PROMETEON TYRE GROUP AND ITS COMMITMENT FOR CLIMATE CHANGE

With its adherence to United Nations Global Compact and supports to the Sustainable Development Goals, Prometeon Tyre Group its actively committed to participate to all activities aimed to respect the 10 principles of the UNGC and 17 SDGs. For this reason, the Company by its production processes, products and activities support the SDG N°13, Climate Action .

<!-- image -->

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

Climate Change is an undeniable fact of the contemporary world, and it leads to abrupt changes in ecosystems of our Blue Planet by contributing to the expansion of drier climate zones, posing serious risks to wellbeing of all species living on Earth.  Moreover, the risks are very likely to cause negative impact on living conditions of human life in all its aspects i.e. economic, social and cultural life.

To manage better Global Climate Change and risks associated to the phenomenon, the Company pays special attention to develop a more comprehensive and strategic approach to the issue. In order to achieve  this  object,  Prometeon  Tyre  Group  adopted  systematic  method  to  identify  and  evaluate eventual future threats that might lead to adverse effects on the environment. Through risk committees established in 2022 at both HQ and local levels in the Countries where business and manufacturing activities take place the actual si tuation is closely monitored. Thanks to the risk committees' meetings on monthly basis, risks of different nature (environmental, technical, physical, regulatory) that might be arisen in the medium to long term future are identified. Risk elements identified are assessed with all their aspects considered and the financial impacts are quantified. In the light of the results of the assessments carefully studied action plans are drafted and measures adopted.

As a heavy industry company, Prometeon Tyre Group is conscious of its responsibilities towards future generations  in  providing  for  a  better  future  through  upmost  respect  for  the  Planet  and  the Environment. Aware of the adverse effects of the emissions and the direct influence of the effects on the  Global  Climate,  the  Company  is  committed to take  its  place  in tackle  the  issue.  In  line  with  its commitment, Prometeon Tyre Group adopts a pro-active approach through its tyre business activities, starting from production of tyres.

Greenhouse gases emissions, energy consumptions, water management and waste generation caused by production activities are among the KPIs closely monitored. In order to minimize the adverse impacts caused  by  production  activities  and  maximize  the  resource  usage  efficiency,  the  Company  defines specific goals and carries out continuously actions (e.g. employee training, sensibilization campaigns, infrastructure and machinery investments) aimed at reaching the goals. For a more detailed description reference  should  be  made  to  the  paragraph ' Environmental  Management  System  and  Factory's Performance' discussed under ' Environmental Dimension ' of the present report.

Having adopted a comprehensive approach to the issue, Prometeon Tyre Group does not limit itself by focusing merely on negative impacts of its ordinary production processes but evaluates also risks with negative impacts on its business activities such as those of physical nature (extreme whether events with potential impacts on the production plants) and regulatory nature with impacts on tyre sector requirements. To this end, the Company collaborates with various national and international sector associations, actively participating to working groups related to various aspects of tyre manufacturing and business. Thanks to its active participation Prometeon Tyre Group monitors closely developments concerning sector specific technical requirements and regulations, enabling itself to anticipate future market needs and standards.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

As the key elements of CO2 and microplastic emissions caused by tyre business, tyre itself and the materials  of  which  it  is  made  are  another  important  factor  that  must  be  taken  into  account  in commitment to fight the climate change. To reduce and eventually neutralize overall negative impacts of tyres on the enviro nment, Prometeon Tyre Group shaped R&amp;D activities around ' 3R ' concept for achieving a target of 100% of sustainable materials used in the overall production process by reducing raw materials and optimizing processes; recycling used  materials  and  introducing  inside  new  tyres materials; and rethinking to new renewable materials and formulations. Moreover, thanks to the '3R' the quality of tyre would be enhanced also in terms of product life performance and retreadability, hence diminishing the need for new products.

For a more complete view on Promete on Tyre Group's R&amp;D activities regarding sustainable product design and  material  use please refer  to  paragraphs ' Product  Safety,  Performance  and  EcoSustainability' discussed under ' Economic Dimension '; ' Biodiversity ' and 'New Materials' paragraphs in 'Environmental  Dimension' ;  and  ' FOCUS:  ETRMA -European  Tyre  And  Rubber  Manufacturers Association ' discussed under ' Governance and Business Ethics ' chapter of this report.
