## METHODOLOGICAL NOTE

## THE PRINCIPLES FOR DEFINING THE CONTENT AND QUALITY OF THE REPORT

This document represents the first edition of Prometeon Tyre Group Sustainability Report in accordance with GRI related to the 2022 financial year (1st January to 31st December), as well as the performance trends of the 2021-2022 two-year period. The Sustainability Report is annually published.

The Prometeon Sustainability Report is meant to report the relevant issues for the Group and its main stakeholders. It is prepared following the  following the Ten Principles of the United Nations Global Compact  and in  accordance  with  'GRI  Sustainability  Reporting  Standards',  the  most  recent  and widespread  non-financial  reporting  standards  defined  by  the  Global  Reporting  Initiative  (GRI), according to  the  'In  accordance  with'  option  which  provides  for the  reporting  of  at  least  one  GRI indicator for each relevant topic plus all the general disclosures of the GRI 2.

This document has been prepared according to the principles on defining the contents of the report suggested by the GRI 1:

- H. Accuracy : the level of detail in the Report is adequate and makes it possible to understand and assess the Group's sustainability performance in the reporting period.
- I. Balance : the information is reported in an unbiased way and provide a fair representation of the organizat ion's negative and positive impacts.
- J. Clarity :  the  choice  of  a  clear  and  accessible  language  and  the  use  of  graphs  and  tables  to represent the performance of the Group make this Report available and easy to understand for stakeholders.
- K. Comparability : the indicators in the Report are shown for the two-years period 2021-2022 and are accompanied by comments on the performance to allow for the comparison of the Group's performance over time.
- L. Completeness : the information provided in the Report, represent the most relevant environmental, social and economic aspects for the Group's activities.
- M. Sustainability context : the performances of the Prometeon Tyre Group discussed in this Report are part of the broader context of the Group's business sustainability.
- N. Timeliness :  the Report takes into account events that occurred after 31 December 2021 that may be significant for the stakeholders when assessing the Group's performance.
- O. Verifiability : the data presented in the document were collected, processed, and validated by the manager of each department with collaboration of a consulting company. The economic data are consistent with those reported in the Group's annual financial report.

## SUSTAINABILITY REPORT

This document is available under the Sustainability section on the website https://www.prometeon.com/IT/en\_US/sustainability . For further information and suggestions please write to: nicolas.marchi@prometeon.com

## SCOPE

The reporting scope includes Prometeon Tyre Group Srl, based in Milan (Italy), Viale Sarca n. 222, and its subsidiaries in which it holds more than 50% ownership. The data is consolidated on a straight-line basis regardless of the percentage of ownership. The data for the Environmental Dimension includes only the four plants where the Group produces, since these are the most significant from in terms of environmental impacts. The factories are two in Brasil (Gravatai; Sant'Andre), one in Turkey (Izmit) and one in Egypt (Alexandria). On the other hand, the data for Social Dimension takes into account the corporate population of the entire Group.

## Data collection and report validation process

The  data  and  information  reported  in  this  document  was  collected  through  interviews  with representatives and data collection forms. The final version of the Sustainability Report is presented to and approved by the Board of Directors.

## Material topics definition

After the identification of current and potential impacts, through the analysis of company external and internal activities and external pressures, they are evaluated based on their significance. Afterwards the impacts are prioritized, resulting into the List of Material Topics.

The relevance assessment of the impacts depends on two variables: severity and likelihood. Severity takes into account the severity of the impact; how widespread the impact is along the Group's value chain  and  how  hard  it  is  to  counteract  or  make  good  the  resulting  harm.  On  the  other  hand  the likelihood express the chance that the impact occurs.

## The calculation methodology

The methodological indications for certain indicators reported in this Sustainability Report are shown below.

## HUMAN RESOURCES

## New employee hires and employee turnover

The number of new hires and leaving employees during the course of the year are indicated respectively as 'incoming' and 'outgoing'. However, it has to be taken into consideration that the Company avails of temporary labor in order to meet the unexpected increases in production volumes as well as to compensate the absence of labor during annual leaves or in cases of sickness leaves. Therefore, the temporary employees have to be detracted from the amounts of incoming and outgoing employees for understanding the actual flows of employees during the course of the year.

The calculation of the Group personnel by geographical area, gender and contract type was carried out with respect to the data received from the various countries.

## Remuneration indices

In determining the Disclosure 2-21, the following elements of fact were taken into account:

- all employees reported under Disclosure 2-7 were taken into consideration;
- for each part-time employee full-time equivalent (FTE) pay rates has been used;
- the remuneration has been calculated taken into account of:
- o theoretical base salary;
- o actual  bonuses,  commissions,  cash  profit-sharing,  and  other  forms  of  variable  cash payments received by employees during the reporting period.
- the highest -paid individual holds the position of General Manager.

## Health and safety indices

Accident rates are calculated as shown below:

- injury  rate is  the  ratio  between  the  total  number  of  accidents  and  the  total  number  of  hours worked, multiplied by 1.000.000;
- the occupational disease rate is  the  ratio  between the total number of confirmed occupational diseases and the total number of working hours, multiplied by 1.000.000;
- severity rate is the total number of serious accidents expressed as a percentage of the total number of labor hours multiplied by 1.000;
- lost days are the calendar days lost due to accident as of the day following that of the accident;
- the recovery time corresponds to the lost time due to the fact that by company policy injured employees cannot return to work with different tasks than those they originally performed.
- the calculation regarding the workers who are not employees is estimated considering 8 hours/day for each worker for 22 days per month per year.

## ENVIRONMENTAL

## Energy consumption

The conversion factors used to uniform energy consumption derive from the Table published by the Intergovernmental Panel on Climate Change (IPCC 2006).

## NOX and SOX Emissions

The emissions are calculated based on hourly emissions measurements made in each plant, which are subsequently calculated on daily and yearly basis.

## Greenhouse gas emissions

Data  on  total  energy  consumed  in  the  factories  are  collected  for  different  energy  carriers  such  as electricity, natural gas, steam and LPG. The collected data are converted into tons of CO2 equivalent using various conversion factors depending on the specific energy carrier (IEA sources for electricity, factors resulting from internal analyses for steam and IPCC sources for the rest of the energy carriers used  by  Prometeon  Tyre  Group.  The  Group  is  planning  the  introduction  of  a  certified  and  more structured  system  for  the  automatic  calculation  of  GHG  emissions  which  also  takes  into  account emission factors annually updated.

The emission factors used to calculate CO2 emissions were determined as follows:

· Direct emissions (Scope 1): the emissions linked to the consumption of resources that are under the direct control of the Group (natural gas, diesel heating fuel, gas, diesel fuel and LPG) were determined based  on  the  emission  factors  reported  in  the  Table  of  national  standards  published  by  the Intergovernmental Panel on Climate Change (IPCC 2006), for the years 2022 and 2021.

· Indirect emissions (Scope 2): indirect emissions are linked to the consumption of pu rchased electricity and district heating; and were calculated using a location and market-based approach. Considering the consumption of electricity, for the related Scope 2 emissions, for each country in which the Group operates, the factors considered were those presented in tables on page 272. Main energy indicators published by International Energy Agency (IEA 2015). When there were several branches in several countries,  the  highest  of  the  emissions  factors  among  these  countries  was  used.  Considering  the consumption of steam, for the related Scope 2 emissions factors resulting from internal analyses were considered.

## Water

The Group evaluate the water stress area by using the on-line tool, used to understand water-related risks and assess exposure to water risk across multiple locations.

## ENTITIES INCLUDED IN THE ORGANIZATION'S SUSTAINABILITY REPORTING

The  Company  counts  17  subsidiaries  in  13  different  countries.  Subsidiaries  are  defined  as  all  the companies over which Prometeon Tyre Group S.r.L., at the same time, holds:

- The power of decision making or the ability to direct relevant activities of the subsidiary, that is activities that have a significant influence on the results of the subsidiary;
- The right to the variable results (positive or negative) resulting from the investment in the entity;
- The capacity to use its own power of decision making to determine the amounts of the results arising from the investment in the entity.
